1. System kaise kaam karta hai

Chaar baatein samajh lein to baqi sab aasaan hai. Shuru karne se pehle ye boxes ek baar parh lein.

1.1 Ek hi kitab

Maqsad: Batata hai ke har report doosri report se kyun milti hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab.

Tareeqa:

  1. Normal screen mein purchase, sale, return, payment, receipt ya expense darj karein.
  2. Jaise hi woh post hota hai (agla box dekhein), system uski double-entry journal entry likh deta hai.
  3. Balance Sheet, Profit & Loss, Trial Balance aur Cash Flow jaisi reports unhi journal entries se jama hoti hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Reports → Balance Sheet kholein; wahan balanced likha hona chahiye.

Zaroori noteAgar koi document post hi nahi hua, ya reverse ho gaya, to woh reports mein nazar nahi aayega. Numbers ghalat lagne ki sab se aam wajah yehi hai.

1.2 Draft, Posted aur Reversed

Maqsad: Batata hai ke purchase ya sale ki teen halatein kaun si hain aur har ek stock aur accounts par kya asar daalti hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab parh sakte hain. Manager banate aur post karte hain; sirf admin reverse, correct ya draft delete kar sakta hai.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases aur Transactions → Sales (Status column).

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft — aap ne Save Purchase ya Save Sale dabaya. Is par invoice number nahi hota aur stock, ledger ya accounts par koi asar nahi hota. Aap ise edit kar sakte hain, post kar sakte hain, ya (admin) delete kar sakta hai.
  2. Posted — aap ne hara Post button dabaya. Invoice number lagta hai, stock hilta hai, customer ya supplier ledger update hota hai aur journal entry likhi jati hai. Ab edit ya delete nahi ho sakta.
  3. Reversed — admin ne posted document reverse kar diya. Asal document reversed ke nishan ke saath record mein rehta hai, aur saath ek cancelling journal entry post hoti hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Status column mein draft ya payment status nazar aata hai; reversed document par reversed badge hota hai.

Zaroori noteReturns, supplier payments, customer receipts, expenses aur POS sales ka draft marhala nahi hota; woh save hote hi asar karte hain. Tafseel har sahulat ke box mein hai.

1.3 Stock ki costing: weighted average

Maqsad: Batata hai ke system stock ki qeemat aur bechay gaye maal ki cost kaise nikalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab.

Tareeqa:

  1. 10 unit 100 ke hisaab se kharidein, phir 10 unit 140 ke hisaab se.
  2. Ab aap ke paas 20 unit hain jin ki qeemat 2,400 hai, yaani har unit ki cost 120.
  3. Ek unit bechein: uski cost 120 hogi. Ye sirf misaal hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Reports → Stock Valuation kholein aur Avg Cost column ko apni kharidari se milayein.

Zaroori noteCosting weighted average hai, FIFO ya LIFO nahi. Batch-tracked items ki cost jahan batch cost maujood ho wahan batch ke hisaab se bhi hoti hai.

1.4 Roles: Staff, Manager aur Admin

Maqsad: Batata hai ke teeno roles kya kar sakte hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab. Admin har shakhs ko Admin → Users mein role deta hai.

Kahan milega: Jo menu item aap istemal nahi kar sakte woh dhundla dikhta hai; us par mouse rakhein to likha aata hai kaunsa role chahiye.

Tareeqa:

  1. Staff Suppliers, Customers, Items, Purchases, Sales aur return pages dekh sakta hai; supplier payments, customer receipts, bulk payments aur bulk receipts darj kar sakta hai; purchase aur sale returns darj kar sakta hai; delivery challans dekh sakta hai; aur (HR) employees aur attendance dekh sakta hai aur leave request de sakta hai.
  2. Manager isi ke saath suppliers, customers, items, purchases aur sales add/edit kar sakta hai; drafts post kar sakta hai; POS aur held bills; stock adjustments, transfers, reconciliations (banana, ginti, finalize, reopen, cancel), labels aur bulk import; purchase orders, quotations, expenses, journal entries, fixed assets, accounts, tamam reports, Dashboard, aur HR, attendance aur payroll (banana aur edit) istemal kar sakta hai.
  3. Admin isi ke saath documents reverse, correct aur delete kar sakta hai; reconciliations approve aur post kar sakta hai; transfers reverse kar sakta hai; fiscal year close aur periods open/close kar sakta hai; users, warehouses, location access, financial accounts, business configuration, tax codes, chart of accounts, audit log, backup aur restore sambhal sakta hai, aur payroll post kar sakta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Us shakhs ke taur par sign in karein aur menu dekhein; dhundle items woh hain jo us role ke liye band hain.

Zaroori noteKuch pages staff kholne deta hai lekin Save par rok deta hai; tab system 'Access denied. Only managers and admins can add sales.' jaisa message dikhata hai.

2. Sign in aur aap ka account

Sign in aur sign out, register karna, email verify karna, password reset aur tabdeel karna, aur aap ka profile.

2.1 Sign In

Maqsad: Aap ko system mein dakhil karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har woh shakhs jis ka account verified ho.

Kahan milega: Sign-in page; sign out hone par oopar daayen Sign In link.

Tareeqa:

  1. Browser mein system ka address kholein.
  2. Apna Email address likhein.
  3. Apna Password likhein.
  4. Sign In dabayein.
  5. Aap Home page par pahunch jate hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Baayen taraf menu aur oopar daayen aap ka naam nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Invalid email or password!Spelling aur Caps Lock check karein. System nahi batata ke dono mein se kaunsa ghalat tha.
Please verify your email before signing in.Verification email kholein, ya Resend Verification Email istemal karein.
Too many login attempts.Chand minute rukein. Aik computer se paanch minute mein paanch koshishen allowed hain.
Zaroori notePassword kisi ko na dein. Aap ki har tabdeeli audit log mein aap ke naam se likhi jati hai.

2.2 Sign Out

Maqsad: Aap ka session khatam karta hai taake is computer par koi aur aap ka account istemal na kare.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har sign-in shuda user.

Kahan milega: Oopar daayen apne naam par click → Sign Out.

Tareeqa:

  1. Oopar daayen apne naam par click karein.
  2. Sign Out chunein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aap sign-in page par pahunchte hain aur 'Signed out successfully!' likha aata hai.

2.3 Sign Up (sirf jab on ho)

Maqsad: Naya shakhs khud ko register kar sakta hai. Ye aam tor par band hota hai; admin account banata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi, lekin sirf jab installation mein self-registration on ho (setting ALLOW_SIGNUP).

Kahan milega: Sign Up page.

Tareeqa:

  1. Name, Email aur Password (kam az kam 6 huroof) likhein.
  2. Sign Up dabayein.
  3. Verification email kholein aur uska link click karein.
  4. Sign in karein. Naye account ka role Staff hota hai jab tak admin badal na de.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message batata hai ke verification email bhej di gayi; link click karne ke baad aap sign in kar sakte hain.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Registration is disabled. Contact the administrator.Self-registration band hai. Admin se account banwayein.
Email ... is already registered!Forgot Password istemal karein.
Password must be at least 6 characters!Lamba password chunein.

2.4 Email verify karna

Maqsad: Tasdeeq karta hai ke email address aap ka hai, taake account istemal ho sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Naya user jise email mili. Admin bhi kisi user ko verified mark kar sakta hai.

Kahan milega: Verification email ke andar ka link.

Tareeqa:

  1. Wo email kholein jis ka subject verify karne ko kehta hai.
  2. Link par click karein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message ka intezar karein.
  4. Sign in karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai email kamyabi se verify ho gayi aur aap sign in kar sakte hain.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Invalid or expired verification link!Link aik ghante tak chalta hai. Resend Verification Email istemal karein.
Email ... is already verified!Kuch karne ki zaroorat nahi; bas sign in karein.

2.5 Verification email dobara bhejna

Maqsad: Naya verification link bhejta hai jab pehla expire ho gaya ho ya aaya hi na ho.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har woh shakhs jis ka account unverified ho.

Kahan milega: Resend Verification Email page.

Tareeqa:

  1. Apna Email likhein.
  2. Resend Verification Email dabayein.
  3. Nayi email kholein aur link click karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai nayi verification email bhej di gayi hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Email ... not found!Spelling check karein ya admin se poochein.
Email ... is already verified!Bas sign in karein.

2.6 Password bhool gaye

Maqsad: Aap ko nayi password chunne ka link email karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har woh shakhs jise apna account email maloom ho.

Kahan milega: Forgot Password page (sign-in page se link).

Tareeqa:

  1. Forgot Password kholein.
  2. Apna Email likhein.
  3. Send Reset Link dabayein.
  4. Email kholein aur link click karein (Reset Password dekhein).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai ke agar email registered hai to reset link bhej diya gaya hai. Spam folder bhi dekhein.

Zaroori noteMessage har soorat mein aik jaisa hota hai, taake koi ye na jaan sake ke kis ka account hai. Link aik ghante tak chalta hai. Paanch minute mein paanch darkhwastein allowed hain.

2.7 Password reset karna (email ke link se)

Maqsad: Forgot Password ke baad nayi password set karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Wo shakhs jise reset email mili.

Kahan milega: Reset email ka link Reset Password page kholta hai.

Tareeqa:

  1. New Password likhein (kam az kam 6 huroof).
  2. Confirm New Password mein dobara likhein.
  3. Reset Password dabayein.
  4. Nayi password se sign in karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai password kamyabi se reset ho gayi aur sign in karne ko kehta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Invalid or expired reset link!Forgot Password se naya link mangwayein.
Passwords do not match!Dono boxes dobara ehtiyat se likhein.

2.8 Mera Profile

Maqsad: Aap ka naam, email aur account verified hai ya nahi dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har sign-in shuda user.

Kahan milega: Oopar daayen apne naam par click → My Profile.

Tareeqa:

  1. Oopar daayen apne naam par click karein.
  2. My Profile chunein.
  3. Account Details card parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Name, Email aur Verified ya Unverified badge nazar aate hain.

Zaroori noteNaam ya email badalne ke liye admin se kahein (Admin → Users → Edit).

2.9 Password badalna

Halat: Maloom masla (code parh kar maloom hua, browser mein azmaya nahi): Change Password form current_password, new_password aur confirm_password bhejta hai, lekin jo page unhein leta hai woh doosre field naam dhundta hai, isliye umeed hai ke 'Name cannot be empty!' aaye aur password na badle. Jab tak theek na ho, password Forgot Password se badlein, ya admin se Admin → Users → Edit mein nayi password set karwayein.

Maqsad: Sign in rehte hue apni password badalne deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har sign-in shuda user. Demo installations mein ye band hota hai.

Kahan milega: My Profile → Change Password card.

Tareeqa:

  1. Apni Current Password likhein.
  2. New Password likhein (kam az kam 6 huroof).
  3. Confirm New Password mein dobara likhein.
  4. Change Password dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Agar aap ki installation par kaam kare to page kamyabi ka message dikhata hai; tasdeeq ke liye sign out kar ke nayi password se sign in karein.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Name cannot be empty!Oopar bayan kiye gaye maloom masle ka mutawaqqe jawab hai; Forgot Password istemal karein.
Password must be at least 6 characters!Lambi password chunein.

3. System mein rasta dhundna

Home page, dashboard, search, notifications, print, export, aur Manual aur About pages.

3.1 Home page

Maqsad: Sign-in ke baad pehla page: live figures aur haliya sargarmi ka mukhtasar jaiza.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab. Is ke kuch links sirf manager ke liye kaam karte hain.

Kahan milega: Oopar baayen TradeFlow naam, ya sign in.

Tareeqa:

  1. Sign in karein, ya oopar baayen brand naam par click karein.
  2. Jaiza parhein: aaj ki sales aur purchases, receivables, payables, reorder level se neeche items, expiry ke qareeb batches.
  3. Haliya sargarmi ki list parhein (aakhri sale ya purchase, mili hui payment, stock transfer, aur kam stock ya expiry ki warnings).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Receivable aur payable figures customer aur supplier ledgers se milte hain.

3.2 Global Search

Maqsad: Kisi bhi page se supplier, customer ya item dhoondta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har sign-in shuda user.

Kahan milega: Har page ke oopar search box: 'Search supplier, customer, item...'.

Tareeqa:

  1. Search box par click karein.
  2. Naam, contact ya address ka kuch hissa likhein.
  3. Neeche aane wali list se result chunein; woh us record ka page kholta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Chuna hua supplier, customer ya item ka page khul jata hai.

3.3 Notifications dekhna

Maqsad: Aap ke naam alerts dikhata hai: kam stock, leave requests aur faislay, payroll finalize ya cancel.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har user sirf apni notifications dekhta hai. Kam stock ke alerts manager aur admin ko; payroll ke alerts admin ko jate hain.

Kahan milega: Oopar daayen ghanti ka nishan → Notifications.

Tareeqa:

  1. Ghanti ke nishan par click karein.
  2. When, Severity, Title aur Message columns parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Parhne par ghanti ka laal ginti kam hoti jati hai.

3.4 Notifications read mark karna

Maqsad: Alert par kaam ho jane ke baad use saaf karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har sign-in shuda user, apni notifications ke liye.

Kahan milega: Notifications page.

Tareeqa:

  1. Kisi aik notification ke saath Mark read dabayein, ya sab saaf karne ke liye oopar Mark all read dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Notification ab ghanti ki ginti mein nahi aati.

Zaroori noteJab tak pehla alert unread hai, usi item ka naya kam-stock alert dobara nahi aata.

3.5 Print ya PDF save karna

Maqsad: Browser ke zariye table ya report print karta hai, ya PDF file ki surat mein save karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har woh shakhs jo page khol sakta hai.

Kahan milega: Aksar lists aur reports ke oopar Print PDF ya Print button.

Tareeqa:

  1. Filters aise lagayein ke page wohi dikhaye jo chahiye.
  2. Print PDF (ya Print) dabayein.
  3. Browser ke print window mein printer chunein, ya destination mein Save as PDF chunein.
  4. Print ya Save dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Preview mein title wali table, company ka naam aur bina action buttons ke nazar aati hai.

Zaroori noteSystem khud PDF file nahi banata; browser banata hai. Sale aur purchase invoices printable page ki surat mein khulte hain; POS receipts 80 mm kaghaz par print hoti hain (POS: receipt print dekhein).

3.6 CSV ya Excel mein export karna

Maqsad: List ya report ko spreadsheet file ki surat mein download karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Item Ledger ke ilawa har export ke liye manager aur admin; Item Ledger koi bhi verified user export kar sakta hai.

Kahan milega: Suppliers, Customers, Item Ledger, Supplier Ledger, Customer Ledger, Stock Valuation aur Reports page par CSV aur Excel buttons.

Tareeqa:

  1. Jo date range ya search chahiye lagayein.
  2. CSV ya Excel dabayein.
  3. Download shuda file spreadsheet program mein kholein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: File mein wohi rows aur totals hote hain jo screen par hain.

3.7 Dashboard

Maqsad: Karobari totals aur charts dikhata hai: payable, receivable, sales aur profit, top items, top suppliers aur customers, payment methods, expenses, aur stock alerts.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Menu mein Dashboard.

Tareeqa:

  1. Dashboard kholein.
  2. Agar sirf aik product category dekhni ho to category filter istemal karein.
  3. Oopar buttons istemal karein: View Reports, Expired Items, Near to Expiry, Low Stock Items, Business Insights.
  4. Payable aur Receivable cards ke neeche Record Payment aur Record Receipt se seedha un screens par jayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Payable aur Receivable cards customer aur supplier ledgers se milte hain.

Zaroori noteFigures sirf posted documents se aate hain; drafts shamil nahi hote.

3.8 Business Insights

Maqsad: Wo customers jin par sab se zyada baqaya hai aur kitni muddat se, wo suppliers jinhein aap ne dena hai, aur kam stock wale items dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Dashboard → Business Insights.

Tareeqa:

  1. Dashboard kholein.
  2. Business Insights dabayein.
  3. Customer (Balance Due, Oldest Sale Age), Supplier aur Item (Current Stock) tables parhein.
  4. Kaam ke baad Back to Dashboard dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Balances customer aur supplier ledgers se milte hain.

3.9 Purchase List banana

Maqsad: Wo items ki shopping list print karta hai jo reorder level se neeche hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Dashboard → Low Stock Items table → Create Purchase List.

Tareeqa:

  1. Dashboard par Low Stock Items tak scroll karein.
  2. Create Purchase List dabayein.
  3. List dekhein, phir Print PDF dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har listed item ka Current Stock Reorder Level ke barabar ya kam hota hai.

3.10 User Manual aur About page kholna

Maqsad: Ye manual, ya system kya karta hai uska mukhtasar bayan parhna.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi, sign in kiye baghair bhi.

Kahan milega: Menu mein Manual aur About.

Tareeqa:

  1. Menu mein Manual (ya About) par click karein.
  2. Manual mein kisi module ya sahulat par jane ke liye Contents list ya sidebar istemal karein.
  3. Jis zaban mein parh rahe hain usi mein manual print karne ke liye oopar Print dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Page English text aur zaban badalne ke switch ke saath khulta hai.

3.11 Manual ya About page ki zaban English aur Roman Urdu mein badalna

Maqsad: Wohi pages English ya angrezi huroof mein likhi Urdu mein dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi.

Kahan milega: Manual aur About pages ke oopar English aur اردو (Roman) buttons.

Tareeqa:

  1. Roman Urdu mein parhne ke liye اردو (Roman) dabayein, ya wapas jane ke liye English.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Text foran zaban badal leta hai.

Zaroori noteHar bar page English mein khulta hai; aap ki chuni hui zaban yaad nahi rakhi jati.

3.12 Lambi lists mein search, sort aur page badalna

Maqsad: Un pages par jahan suppliers, customers, items, purchases, sales waghera ki list ho, records jaldi dhoondta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Har woh shakhs jo page khol sakta hai.

Kahan milega: List ke oopar search box, column headings, aur table ke neeche page links.

Tareeqa:

  1. List ke search box mein naam ka hissa likhein aur Search dabayein; sab dobara dekhne ke liye Clear (jahan ho) dabayein.
  2. Suppliers, Customers aur Items mein sort ke liye column heading (maslan Name ya Opening) par click karein; order ulta karne ke liye dobara click karein.
  3. Agle page ke liye table ke neeche page links istemal karein; list mein har page par das rows hoti hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Table sirf matching rows aap ke chune hue order mein dikhati hai.

Zaroori noteSuppliers aur Customers pages ki total row mojooda search se milti rows ka total hoti hai, sirf nazar aane wala page nahi.

4. Users aur ijazatein

Sign in karne wale logon ko shamil aur manage karna. Admin menu: Users. Ye pages sirf admin khol sakta hai.

4.1 Users dekhna

Maqsad: Har user ko role aur verified status ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Users kholein.
  2. #, Name, Email, Role, Status aur Actions columns parhein.
  3. List print karne ke liye Print PDF dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aap ki apni row par You badge hota hai.

4.2 User banana

Maqsad: Naya shakhs shamil karta hai jo sign in kar sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users → Create User

Tareeqa:

  1. Create User dabayein.
  2. Full Name aur Email Address likhein.
  3. Password (kam az kam 6 huroof) likhein aur Confirm Password mein dobara likhein.
  4. Role chunein: Staff, Manager ya Admin.
  5. Agar shakhs foran sign in kar sake to Mark as Verified par tick karein.
  6. Create User dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai user kamyabi se ban gaya aur user list mein nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Name, email, and password are required.Teeno bharein.
Passwords do not match.Dono boxes dobara likhein.
Email ... is already registered.Doosra email istemal karein ya maujooda user edit karein.
Password must be at least 6 characters.Lambi password chunein.
Zaroori notePage par Role Permissions table bhi hoti hai. Wo sab se chhota role chunein jis se shakhs apna kaam kar sake.

4.3 User edit karna

Maqsad: User ka naam, email, password, role ya verified flag badalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users → row par pencil button

Tareeqa:

  1. Pencil (Edit user) button dabayein.
  2. Zaroorat ke mutabiq Full Name, Email Address ya Role badlein.
  3. New Password sirf tab likhein jab badalni ho; khali chhodein to purani rehti hai.
  4. Verified par tick lagayein ya hataein.
  5. Save Changes dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai user kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Name and email are required.Dono bharein.
Email ... is already in use.Doosra email chunein.
Password must be at least 6 characters.Lambi password chunein.

4.4 User ka role badalna

Maqsad: Shakhs ko Staff, Manager aur Admin ke darmiyan jaldi muntaqil karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users → row ka role dropdown

Tareeqa:

  1. Shakhs ki row dhoondein.
  2. Dropdown mein naya role chunein.
  3. Hare tick (Save role) ko dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message likhta hai '<name>'s role changed from ... to ...'.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
You cannot change your own role.Kisi doosre admin se kahein.
Invalid role.Staff, Manager ya Admin chunein.

4.5 User ko verified ya unverified karna

Maqsad: Account delete kiye baghair sign-in ki ijazat dene ya rokne deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users → row ka verify button

Tareeqa:

  1. Row ka button dabayein: unverified user ke liye Mark verified aur verified ke liye Mark unverified likha hota hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Status column badal jata hai aur message batata hai ke user ab verified ya unverified hai.

Zaroori noteUnverified user ko email verify karne ko kaha jata hai aur woh system istemal nahi kar sakta.

4.6 User delete karna

Maqsad: User account hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Users → row par trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Trash button dabayein.
  2. Sawal ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai user delete ho gaya aur row ghayab ho jati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
You cannot delete your own account.Kisi doosre admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteAgar sirf sign in rokna ho to unverified mark karein; ye wapas ho sakta hai.

5. Warehouses aur location access

Stock kahan rakha jata hai aur har user kaun se warehouses istemal kar sakta hai. Admin menu.

5.1 Warehouse shamil karna

Maqsad: Stock ki nayi jagah banata hai. Purchases, sales, transfers, adjustments aur ginti sab warehouse ka naam lete hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Warehouses → Add a warehouse

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Warehouses kholein.
  2. Name likhein (sirf misaal: 'Karachi Store').
  3. Chaahein to Address likhein.
  4. Add Warehouse dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Naya warehouse table aur Warehouse drop-downs mein nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Warehouse name is required.Naam likhein.
A warehouse named ... already exists.Doosra naam rakhein.
Zaroori noteSirf aik warehouse ho to Warehouse Transfers menu chhupa rehta hai. Doosra warehouse banne par nazar aata hai.

5.2 Warehouse band ya dobara chalu karna

Maqsad: Warehouse ko naye documents ke liye pesh hone se rokta hai, ya wapas laata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Warehouses → Action column mein Deactivate / Reactivate

Tareeqa:

  1. Warehouse dhoondein.
  2. Deactivate dabayein (ghair-faal ke liye Reactivate).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Status column badal jata hai aur message batata hai ke warehouse ab active ya inactive hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... is the default warehouse and cannot be deactivated.Default warehouse (Default badge wala) locked hota hai.

5.3 User ko warehouse ka access dena

Maqsad: Manager ya staff user ko mukhtas warehouses tak mehdood karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Location Access

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Location Access kholein.
  2. User ki row dhoondein.
  3. Grant a warehouse mein warehouse chunein.
  4. Grant dabayein. Har us warehouse ke liye dohrayein jo user istemal kar sakta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message likhta hai '<user> may now access <warehouse>' aur row mein woh nazar aata hai.

Zaroori noteJis user ko koi grant nahi, woh har warehouse istemal kar sakta hai. Pehli grant se user sirf granted warehouses tak reh jata hai. Admin kabhi mehdood nahi hota aur is page par nazar nahi aata.

5.4 User ka warehouse access wapas lena

Maqsad: User se aik granted warehouse hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Location Access

Tareeqa:

  1. User ki row dhoondein.
  2. Jo warehouse hatana ho uske chhote gol x (Revoke) button ko dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Access revoked.'

Zaroori noteAakhri grant hatane se user dobara unrestricted ho jata hai, yaani har warehouse ka access.

6. Cash aur bank accounts, aur business configuration

Paisa kahan rakha jata hai, aur aap ke karobar ki product categories aur fields. Admin menu.

6.1 Cash ya Bank account shamil karna

Maqsad: Aisa account banata hai jis mein payments, receipts, expenses aur POS sales post ho sakein.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Financial Accounts → Add Account

Tareeqa:

  1. Add Account dabayein.
  2. Account Type chunein: Control (heading, jis mein kabhi post nahi hota), Subsidiary (control account ke neeche) ya Standalone.
  3. Account Name likhein (misal: 'UBL').
  4. Jahan page de wahan Account Subtype (Cash ya Bank) chunein.
  5. Subsidiary ke liye Parent Control Account chunein.
  6. Chaahein to Opening Balance likhein.
  7. Create Account dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Account apne control heading ya Standalone Accounts ke neeche nazar aata hai, aur payment, receipt aur expense screens ke Account drop-down mein bhi.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Account name is required!Naam likhein.
An account named ... already exists!Naam unique hona chahiye, capitals aur spaces nazar-andaz hote hain.
A subsidiary account must belong to a control account!Parent Control Account chunein.
Invalid opening balance!Number likhein.
Zaroori noteOpening balance asli accounting entry hai. Karobar shuru hone se pehle darj karein.

6.2 Cash ya Bank account edit ya dobara chalu karna

Maqsad: Account ka naam, jagah ya opening balance badalta hai, ya ghair-faal account wapas laata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Financial Accounts → Edit (pencil) ya Inactive Accounts ke neeche Edit / Reactivate

Tareeqa:

  1. Account ke saath Edit dabayein.
  2. Fields badlein.
  3. Save button dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai account kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
An account cannot be its own parent!Koi doosra parent control account chunein.
An account named ... already exists!Unique naam rakhein.

6.3 Cash ya Bank account delete karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se bane account ko hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Financial Accounts → trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Account ke saath trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Delete ...?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai account kamyabi se delete ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete account ... it has subsidiary accounts!Pehle subsidiaries delete ya muntaqil karein.
Cannot delete account ... it has transactions!Isay edit kar ke inactive karein.

6.4 Business Categories: dekhna, enable aur disable

Maqsad: Aap ke karobar ki product categories dikhata hai aur har ek ko on ya off karne deta hai. Item ka enabled business category mein hona zaroori hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Business Configuration kholein.
  2. Category card par Enabled / Disabled switch istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Items page ke Category drop-down mein sirf enabled categories nazar aati hain.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
No business categories are enabled. Please enable categories in Admin Settings before adding items!Yahan kam az kam aik category enable karein.

6.5 Business Category shamil karna

Maqsad: Nayi product category banata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration → Add Category

Tareeqa:

  1. Add Category dabayein.
  2. Category Name likhein (misal: 'Clothing').
  3. Chaahein to Description likhein.
  4. Create Category dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Nayi card list mein nazar aati hai.

6.6 Business Category edit karna

Maqsad: Category ka naam ya description badalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration → category card par Edit

Tareeqa:

  1. Edit dabayein.
  2. Naam ya description badlein.
  3. Update Category dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Card par naya naam nazar aata hai.

6.7 Category par Batch Tracking aur Expiry on karna

Maqsad: Tay karta hai ke is category ke items batch ke hisaab se track hon aur expiry date rakhein (maslan dawaiyan).

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration → category card par Fields → Stock tracking

Tareeqa:

  1. Category par Fields dabayein.
  2. Stock tracking mein Batch tracking aur/ya Stock expiry par tick lagayein.
  3. Tabdeeli foran save ho jati hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Add Item form mein ye category chunne par Batch tracked option us par lagoo hota hai.

Zaroori noteMaujooda item tab hi batch-tracked hota hai jab aap uske Edit Item page par Enable Batch Tracking dabayen.

6.8 Category mein Product Field shamil karna

Maqsad: Us category ke item form mein ekstra box (maslan Size ya Colour) shamil karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration → Fields → Add Field

Tareeqa:

  1. Category par Fields, phir Add Field dabayein.
  2. Field Label (jo log dekhte hain) aur Field Name (code) likhein.
  3. Field Type chunein: Text, Number, Date, Dropdown, Yes/No ya Textarea.
  4. Chaahein to Tab Name, Required field? aur Searchable? set karein.
  5. Save button dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Field table mein aur us category ke item form mein nazar aati hai.

6.9 Product Field delete karna

Maqsad: Category se ekstra field hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Business Configuration → Fields → Delete

Tareeqa:

  1. Category ka Fields page kholein.
  2. Field ke saath Delete dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Field table se ghayab ho jati hai.

7. Audit, backup, reset aur developer tools

Audit log, data mehfooz aur restore karna, naye sire se shuru karna, aur technical Developer Tasks panel.

7.1 Audit Log

Maqsad: Kis ne kab kya kiya, uska record.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Audit Log

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Audit Log kholein.
  2. Chaahein to Filter: mein koi action chunein taake sirf usi qisam ki entries nazar aayein.
  3. When, User, Action, Entity aur Details parhein.
  4. Page print karne ke liye Print PDF dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Sab se nayi entries oopar hoti hain.

Zaroori notePage par edit ya delete buttons nahi hain. Export ki sahulat nahi; Print PDF istemal karein.

7.2 Backup download karna

Maqsad: Har table ko aik JSON file mein mehfooz karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Backup & Restore → Backup Database

Tareeqa:

  1. Admin → Backup & Restore kholein.
  2. Download Backup (.json) dabayein.
  3. File aisi jagah rakhein jo sirf is computer ki aik disk na ho.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aik .json file download hoti hai. Page har table aur uski rows ki ginti bhi dikhata hai.

Zaroori noteHar hafte aur kisi bhi na-qabil-e-wapsi kaam se pehle backup lein.

7.3 Backup se restore karna

Maqsad: System ka sab kuch backup file ke mawad se badal deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Backup & Restore → Restore Database

Tareeqa:

  1. Pehle taaza backup download karein.
  2. Select backup file ke neeche file box dabayein aur .json backup chunein.
  3. Restore Now dabayein (browser pooche to tasdeeq karein).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai database kamyabi se restore ho gaya aur rows ki ginti batata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
No file selected.File chunein.
Invalid file — not a valid JSON backup.Download Backup se bani hui file istemal karein.
Restore failed (no changes were applied).Kuch nahi badla; file check kar ke dobara koshish karein.
Zaroori noteRestore maujooda data ko badal deta hai. Backup ke baad jo kuch darj hua woh zaya ho jata hai.

7.4 Tamam Transactions delete karna

Maqsad: Karobari tareekh saaf karta hai lekin wo log aur products rakhta hai jin ke saath karobar hota hai, maslan trial khatam ho kar asli karobar shuru hone par.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Backup & Restore → Delete Transactions

Tareeqa:

  1. Pehle backup download karein.
  2. Tasdeeqi box mein bilkul DELETE ALL TRANSACTIONS likhein.
  3. Delete Transactions dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai tamam transactions delete ho gaye aur batata hai ke kitne suppliers, customers aur items bache.

Zaroori noteBachta hai: users, suppliers, customers, items, categories, expense categories, chart of accounts, tax codes, fiscal years aur periods, aur cash/bank accounts. Saaf hota hai: invoices, payments, journals, assets aur doosre records, aur suppliers, customers aur items ke opening balances aur stock sifar ho jate hain. Invoice numbering dobara shuru hoti hai. Band fiscal year dobara khul jata hai.
Zaroori noteYe wapas nahi ho sakta.

7.5 Sab kuch delete karna

Maqsad: System se tamam karobari data saaf karta hai taake kisi aur ko diya ja sake jo apna data khud darj kare.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Admin → Backup & Restore → Delete Everything

Tareeqa:

  1. Pehle backup download karein.
  2. Tasdeeqi box mein bilkul DELETE ALL DATA likhein.
  3. Delete Everything dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai tamam karobari data delete ho gaya aur system khali hai aur setup ke liye tayyar.

Zaroori noteSirf user accounts bachte hain. Standard chart of accounts, tax codes, is saal ke periods aur chaar cash/bank accounts dobara ban jate hain; baqi sab kuch, bamu-e-warehouses, business categories aur HR records, dobara setup karna hoga.
Zaroori noteYe wapas nahi ho sakta.

7.6 Developer Tasks panel

Maqsad: Installation sambhalne wale shakhs ke liye password-protected technical hissa: database tools, diagnostics, logs, environment settings aur optional modules.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf technical staff. Ye tab hi kaam karta hai jab installation mein panel on ho (ENABLE_DEVELOPER_PANEL), aam tor par sirf local machine tak mehdood hota hai, developer password maangta hai, aur kuch der khamoshi ke baad sign out kar deta hai.

Kahan milega: Oopar daayen apne naam par click → Developer Tasks.

Tareeqa:

  1. Apne naam ke menu se Developer Tasks chunein.
  2. Developer Password likhein aur Unlock Developer Panel dabayein.
  3. Tool chunein: Database Manager, Schema Sync, Data Display, Diagnostics, Statistics, Logs, Optional Modules ya Environment.
  4. Kaam ke baad Logout dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Sahi password ke baad Developer Tasks Panel nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Incorrect password.Dobara likhein.
Too many failed attempts. Try again in N minutes.Intezar karein; paanch ghalat koshishon par panel 15 minute ke liye band ho jata hai.
The page is refused or not found.Panel band hai ya sirf server computer tak mehdood hai.
Zaroori noteEnvironment ya database settings yahan tab tak na badlein jab tak aap ko theek maloom na ho; ye sab ko mutassir karti hain.

7.7 HR, Attendance, Leave aur Payroll modules on ya off karna

Maqsad: Optional HR menus dikhata ya chhupata hai. Chaaron by-default off hote hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Developer Tasks panel istemal karne wala technical staff.

Kahan milega: Developer Tasks → Optional Modules

Tareeqa:

  1. Optional Modules kholein.
  2. Pehle HR Module on karein; Attendance, Leave aur Payroll sab isi par munhasir hain.
  3. Jo doosre chahiye woh on karein.
  4. Page reload karein; HR & Payroll menu nazar aata hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Module ka status ON hota hai aur uske menu items un users ko nazar aate hain jinhein ijazat hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Turn HR Module on first — ... depends on it.Doosron se pehle HR on karein.
Zaroori noteModule off karne se uske menu chhup jate hain aur pages band ho jate hain; koi record delete nahi hota, aur post ho chuki payroll journal entries kitabon mein rehti hain.

7.8 Developer Tasks: Environment settings

Maqsad: Installation ki settings dikhata hai, maslan application ka naam, company ka naam, time zone, currency, sign-up ki ijazat, fiscal year ka aaghaz ka mahina aur mail server, saath database mein mehfooz tabdeel hone wali configuration values.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Developer Tasks panel istemal karne wala technical staff.

Kahan milega: Developer Tasks → Environment

Tareeqa:

  1. Developer Tasks panel mein Environment kholein.
  2. Settings parhein; database ka address chhupa hota hai.
  3. Tabdeel hone wali configuration value badalne ke liye usay edit kar ke save karein; read-only values nahi badalti.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Configuration <name> updated to <value>' likhta hai, aur agar restart zaroori ho to warning ke saath.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Configuration <name> is read-only.Ye value yahan nahi badal sakti.
Configuration <name> not found.Page dobara load karein.
Zaroori noteCompany ka naam, currency, time zone aur fiscal year ka aaghaz ka mahina system install karte waqt tay hote hain. Data darj karne se pehle chunein: fiscal year ka aaghaz ka mahina baad mein badalne se pehle se maujood fiscal years nahi hilte.

8. Suppliers aur Customers

Har supplier aur customer ka list page, ledger, aur apni payments ya receipts hoti hain (Buying aur Selling dekhein).

8.1 Suppliers dekhna aur dhoondna

Maqsad: Har Supplier ko opening balance, bills, paid aur balance ke saath dikhata hai, aakhir mein total row ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekh sakta hai; sirf manager aur admin add ya tabdeel kar sakte hain.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers

Tareeqa:

  1. Parties → Suppliers kholein.
  2. Search box mein naam, contact ya address ka hissa likhein aur Search dabayein.
  3. Sort karne ke liye column heading (ID, Name, Contact, Address, Opening) par click karein.
  4. List print karne ke liye Print dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Neeche ki row mojooda rows ke totals dikhati hai.

Zaroori noteBills is supplier ke posted purchases ka total hai. Balance wo hai jo abhi dena baqi hai.

8.2 Supplier shamil karna

Maqsad: Naya Supplier banata hai, chaahein to system istemal karne se pehle ke balance ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers → Add New Supplier

Tareeqa:

  1. Name, Contact aur Address bharein (teeno zaroori).
  2. Agar pehle ka balance tha to Opening Balance likhein (jo aap ne unhein dena hai).
  3. Add Supplier dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Supplier kamyabi se shamil ho gaya aur row list mein nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
All fields are required!Name, contact aur address bharein.
Enter a valid phone number (7-15 digits; + - ( ) and spaces are fine).Contact durust karein.
Supplier already exists!Maujooda record search karein.
Opening balance must be a valid number!Number likhein.
You do not have permission to add suppliers.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteOpening balance books mein Opening Balance Equity ke muqable post hota hai, isliye Balance Sheet aur Supplier ke ledger mein nazar aata hai. Transactions se pehle darj karein.

8.3 Supplier edit karna

Maqsad: Supplier ka naam, contact, address ya opening balance durust karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers → row ka pencil button

Tareeqa:

  1. Pencil (Edit supplier) button dabayein.
  2. Fields badlein.
  3. Update Supplier dabayein (ya bina save kiye Cancel).
  4. View Full Ledger ledger kholta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Supplier kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
All fields are required!Har field bharein.
Opening balance must be a valid number!Number likhein.
Zaroori noteOpening balance badalne se purani opening entry reverse hoti hai aur nayi post hoti hai, isliye do baar nahi gini jati.

8.4 Supplier delete karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se darj hue Supplier ko hata deta hai jis ki koi tareekh nahi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers → row par trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Delete this supplier?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Supplier kamyabi se delete ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete supplier with associated purchases!Record ki tareekh hai; use rehne dein.
Cannot delete supplier with associated payments!Pehle unhein delete ya reverse karein, ya record rehne dein.

8.5 Supplier list export karna

Maqsad: Supplier list ko CSV ya Excel ki surat mein download karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers → CSV / Excel buttons

Tareeqa:

  1. Parties → Suppliers kholein.
  2. CSV ya Excel dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aik file download hoti hai jis mein har Supplier ki aik row hoti hai.

8.6 Supplier Ledger: dekhna aur filter karna

Maqsad: Aik Supplier ki har entry dikhata hai: date, type, description, debit, credit aur chalta hua balance, Total row ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekh sakta hai; sirf manager aur admin add ya tabdeel kar sakte hain.

Kahan milega: Parties → Suppliers → row ka book button (View ledger)

Tareeqa:

  1. List se ledger kholein.
  2. Aik muddat dekhne ke liye Start Date aur End Date chunein aur Filter dabayein.
  3. Print karne ke liye Print PDF dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aakhri Balance Supplier list ke balance ke barabar hota hai (poori date range ke liye).

Zaroori noteBalance wo hai jo aap ne unhein dena hai.

8.7 Supplier Ledger mein Manual Adjustment lagana

Maqsad: Jab koi document na ho to is ledger mein seedha durusti ki line lagata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Supplier Ledger → Manual Adjustment

Tareeqa:

  1. Ledger kholein.
  2. Manual Adjustment mein Date chunein.
  3. Type chunein: Credit (Bill) jo dena hai usme izafa karta hai; Debit (Payment) usay kam karta hai.
  4. Amount aur chaahein to Description likhein.
  5. Add dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Ledger adjustment added!' kehta hai aur Adjustment type ki line nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Valid date, type and amount are required for adjustment!Date, type aur amount bharein.
Access denied. Only managers and admins can add ledger adjustments.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteManual adjustment sirf is Supplier ke ledger ko badalti hai; koi journal entry post nahi hoti. General ledger ise nahi dekhta, isliye Subledger Reconciliation report mein farq aa sakta hai. Sirf aakhri chara ke tor par istemal karein.

8.8 Supplier Ledger se Manual Adjustment hatana

Maqsad: Ghalti se lagi adjustment line hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Supplier Ledger → Adjustment line ka delete button

Tareeqa:

  1. Ledger kholein.
  2. Adjustment line ka delete button dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Adjustment deleted!' kehta hai aur balance dobara hisaab hota hai.

Zaroori noteIs tareeqe se sirf Adjustment lines delete hoti hain. Purchases, sales, payments aur receipts ledger se kabhi delete nahi hote.

8.9 Supplier Ledger export karna

Maqsad: Aik ledger ko CSV ya Excel mein download karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Supplier Ledger → CSV / Excel

Tareeqa:

  1. Ledger kholein, date filter lagayein.
  2. CSV ya Excel dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Download shuda file screen se milti hai.

8.10 Customers dekhna aur dhoondna

Maqsad: Har Customer ko opening balance, sales, received aur balance ke saath dikhata hai, aakhir mein total row ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekh sakta hai; sirf manager aur admin add ya tabdeel kar sakte hain.

Kahan milega: Parties → Customers

Tareeqa:

  1. Parties → Customers kholein.
  2. Search box mein naam, contact ya address ka hissa likhein aur Search dabayein.
  3. Sort karne ke liye column heading (ID, Name, Contact, Address, Opening) par click karein.
  4. List print karne ke liye Print dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Neeche ki row mojooda rows ke totals dikhati hai.

Zaroori noteSales sale returns minus karne ke baad ka net figure hai (active sales par returns ghatane ke baad); ye gross sales nahi hai. Balance wo hai jo customer ne abhi dena hai.

8.11 Customer shamil karna

Maqsad: Naya Customer banata hai, chaahein to system istemal karne se pehle ke balance ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Customers → Add New Customer

Tareeqa:

  1. Name, Contact aur Address bharein (teeno zaroori).
  2. Agar pehle ka balance tha to Opening Balance likhein (jo unhon ne aap ko dena hai).
  3. Add Customer dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Customer kamyabi se shamil ho gaya aur row list mein nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
All fields are required!Name, contact aur address bharein.
Enter a valid phone number (7-15 digits; + - ( ) and spaces are fine).Contact durust karein.
Customer already exists!Maujooda record search karein.
Opening balance must be a valid number!Number likhein.
You do not have permission to add customers.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteOpening balance books mein Opening Balance Equity ke muqable post hota hai, isliye Balance Sheet aur Customer ke ledger mein nazar aata hai. Transactions se pehle darj karein.

8.12 Customer edit karna

Maqsad: Customer ka naam, contact, address ya opening balance durust karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Customers → row ka pencil button

Tareeqa:

  1. Pencil (Edit customer) button dabayein.
  2. Fields badlein.
  3. Update Customer dabayein (ya bina save kiye Cancel).
  4. View Full Ledger ledger kholta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Customer kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
All fields are required!Har field bharein.
Opening balance must be a valid number!Number likhein.
Zaroori noteOpening balance badalne se purani opening entry reverse hoti hai aur nayi post hoti hai, isliye do baar nahi gini jati.

8.13 Customer delete karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se darj hue Customer ko hata deta hai jis ki koi tareekh nahi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Customers → row par trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Delete this customer?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Customer kamyabi se delete ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete customer with associated sales!Record ki tareekh hai; use rehne dein.
Cannot delete customer with associated receipts!Pehle unhein delete ya reverse karein, ya record rehne dein.

8.14 Customer list export karna

Maqsad: Customer list ko CSV ya Excel ki surat mein download karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Parties → Customers → CSV / Excel buttons

Tareeqa:

  1. Parties → Customers kholein.
  2. CSV ya Excel dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aik file download hoti hai jis mein har Customer ki aik row hoti hai.

8.15 Customer Ledger: dekhna aur filter karna

Maqsad: Aik Customer ki har entry dikhata hai: date, type, description, debit, credit aur chalta hua balance, Total row ke saath.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekh sakta hai; sirf manager aur admin add ya tabdeel kar sakte hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Customer Ledger → Manual Adjustment

Tareeqa:

  1. Ledger kholein.
  2. Manual Adjustment mein Date chunein.
  3. Type chunein: Debit (Sale) jo unhon ne dena hai usme izafa karta hai; Credit (Receipt) usay kam karta hai.
  4. Amount aur chaahein to Description likhein.
  5. Add dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Ledger adjustment added!' kehta hai aur Adjustment type ki line nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Valid date, type and amount are required for adjustment!Date, type aur amount bharein.
Access denied. Only managers and admins can add ledger adjustments.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteManual adjustment sirf is Customer ke ledger ko badalti hai; koi journal entry post nahi hoti. General ledger ise nahi dekhta, isliye Subledger Reconciliation report mein farq aa sakta hai. Sirf aakhri chara ke tor par istemal karein.

8.17 Customer Ledger se Manual Adjustment hatana

Maqsad: Ghalti se lagi adjustment line hata deta hai.

9. Inventory: items, batches aur labels

Items banana aur sambhalna, batch aur expiry tracking, bulk import aur labels. Menu: Inventory.

9.1 Items dekhna aur dhoondna

Maqsad: Har item ko unit, barcode, SKU, opening stock, current stock, reorder level aur qeematon ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekh sakta hai; sirf manager aur admin add ya tabdeel kar sakte hain.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items (page ka title Inventory hai)

Tareeqa:

  1. Inventory → Items kholein.
  2. Search box mein likhein aur/ya business category chunein, phir Search dabayein.
  3. Sort ke liye column heading par click karein.
  4. Print PDF se print karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Current Stock Stock Valuation report se milta hai.

9.2 Item shamil karna

Maqsad: Aisa product ya service banata hai jo aap bechte ya kharidte hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → Add New Item

Tareeqa:

  1. Item Name likhein aur Category chunein (sirf enabled business categories milti hain).
  2. Unit of Measure aur Item Type chunein: Stock Item (stock ginta jata hai) ya Service Item (inventory nahi).
  3. Stock item ke liye Opening Stock (aaj shelf par jo hai) aur Reorder Level likhein.
  4. Har unit ki Purchase Price aur Sale Price likhein. Opening stock ke liye purchase price sifar se zyada honi chahiye.
  5. Chaahein to Barcode / QR Code aur SKU, aur dikhne wali Category-Specific Fields likhein.
  6. Add Item dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai item kamyabi se shamil ho gaya; item list mein opening stock ke saath Current Stock nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Name and Category are required!Dono bharein.
Reorder Level is required for Stock items!Likhein.
Opening stock requires a purchase price greater than 0!Har unit ki cost likhein.
Barcode '...' is already used by another item.Barcode aur SKU unique hone chahiye.
No business categories are enabled.Admin Admin → Business Configuration mein ek enable kare.
Zaroori noteOpening stock default warehouse mein jata hai aur aap ki di hui purchase price par value hota hai. Miqdar item ki base unit mein ginti jati hai.

9.3 Batch-tracked item batch-wise opening stock ke saath shamil karna

Maqsad: Dawaiyon jaisay maal ke liye: opening stock batch ke hisaab se expiry dates ke saath darj karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin. Category mein batch tracking on honi chahiye.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → Add New Item → Batch tracked

Tareeqa:

  1. Item Add an Item ki tarah bharein.
  2. Batch tracked (medicines / items with expiry) par tick karein.
  3. Har batch ke liye Add Batch dabayein aur Batch No, Expiry Date, Qty aur Purchase Rate bharein. Bina number ke stock ke liye Batch No khali chhodein (ye Unknown batch ban jata hai).
  4. Add Item dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Stock Valuation item ko batches link ke saath dikhata hai jis mein har batch, warehouse, expiry aur miqdar hoti hai.

Zaroori noteItem ka opening stock batch rows ka total hota hai. Bechte waqt system khud wo batch istemal karta hai jo pehle expire hota hai; expired batches is khud-kaar intikhab mein chhod di jati hain.

9.4 Item mein Alternate Units shamil karna

Maqsad: Aik item ko aik se zyada unit mein kharidne ya bechne deta hai, maslan aik hi dawai ka box aur strip.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → Add New Item ya Edit Item → Alternate Units

Tareeqa:

  1. Alternate Units mein Add Unit dabayein.
  2. Unit Name likhein (misal: Box).
  3. = Base Units mein likhein ke ye kitni base units ke barabar hai (misal: 10).
  4. Chaahein to us unit ki Purchase Price aur Sale Price likhein.
  5. Add Item ya Update Item dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Us item ki Purchase, Sale, Quotation aur Purchase Order lines mein Unit dropdown nazar aata hai.

Zaroori noteStock hamesha base unit mein rehta hai; doosri unit ki line factor se tabdeel hoti hai. Returns asal line ki unit mein wapas hoti hain.

9.5 Item edit karna

Maqsad: Item ka naam, category, qeematen, barcode, SKU, reorder level, unit ya opening stock durust karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → pencil button (Edit item)

Tareeqa:

  1. Item par pencil button dabayein.
  2. Fields badlein. Current Balance Stock sirf maloomat ke liye hota hai.
  3. Update Item dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai item kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot edit opening stock after transactions have been recorded!Stock adjustment istemal karein.
Barcode '...' is already used by another item.Unique code istemal karein.
This item is batch-tracked: its stock is held batch by batch...Batch ki miqdar purchase ya stock adjustment se badlein.

9.6 Maujooda item par Batch Tracking on karna

Maqsad: Aam item ko batch tracking mein tabdeel karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin. Item ki category mein batch tracking on honi chahiye.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → Edit Item → Batch/Lot Tracking

Tareeqa:

  1. Item ko Edit item se kholein.
  2. Batch/Lot Tracking mein Enable Batch Tracking dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message batata hai item par batch tracking on ho gayi aur maujooda stock ka kya hua.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Batch tracking is not enabled for the '...' category.Pehle admin Admin → Business Configuration → Fields mein on kare.
Zaroori noteYe item ko batches mein bechne se pehle karein; maujooda stock ke saath kya hua ye agla message parh kar dekhein.

9.7 Item delete karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se bane item ko hata deta hai jo kabhi kharida ya becha nahi gaya.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Item par trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Are you sure?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai item kamyabi se delete ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete item with associated purchases or sales!Item rehne dein; iski tareekh hai.

9.8 Item Ledger

Maqsad: Aik item ki har harkat dikhata hai: date, type, reference, party, batch, rate, stock in, stock out, value aur chalta balance, aur ye bhi ke item abhi kis warehouse mein hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → book button (View ledger)

Tareeqa:

  1. Item par View ledger dabayein.
  2. Har warehouse ka stock Where This Item Is Right Now mein dekhein.
  3. Start Date aur End Date chunein aur Filter dabayein; reset ke liye Clear.
  4. CSV, Excel ya Print istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aakhri Balance item ke Current Stock ke barabar hota hai.

9.9 Kam stock ka alert email bhejna

Maqsad: Reorder level par ya us se neeche items ki list email karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Items → Low Stock Alert

Tareeqa:

  1. Items page ke oopar Low Stock Alert dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Low stock alert sent for N item(s).'

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Email not configured — cannot send alert.Installation sambhalne wale se mail account set karwayein.

9.10 Items, Customers ya Suppliers ek saath import karna

Maqsad: CSV, Excel ya JSON file se bohat se records ek saath shamil karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin (staff ke liye menu item dhundla hota hai).

Kahan milega: Inventory → Bulk Import

Tareeqa:

  1. Inventory → Bulk Import kholein.
  2. What do you want to import? mein Items / Products, Customers ya Suppliers chunein.
  3. Choose File dabayein aur .csv, .xlsx, .xls ya .json file chunein jis ke columns page ki template misaalon se milte hon.
  4. Import Now dabayein.
  5. Recent Imports parhein; nakaam rows aur wajah dekhne ke liye row par View errors dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Recent Imports aap ki file ke kamyab aur nakaam rows ki ginti dikhata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
File is empty.Dekhein file mein header row aur data hai.
No file selected.Pehle file chunein.
Import failed.Apne columns ko template misaalon se milayein.
Zaroori noteFile mein zyada se zyada 10,000 rows ho sakti hain. Items ke liye stock column opening stock bhi banta hai aur uski opening value (purchase_price par) bhi; category zaroori hai.

9.11 Jin items ka code nahi unhein code dena

Maqsad: Har us item ko unique code deta hai jis ka barcode nahi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Print Labels → Assign codes automatically

Tareeqa:

  1. Inventory → Print Labels kholein.
  2. Assign codes automatically dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message likhta hai 'Assigned a code to N item(s) that had none.'

9.12 Item Labels print karna

Maqsad: Shelves aur products ke liye barcode ya QR labels print karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Print Labels

Tareeqa:

  1. Inventory → Print Labels kholein.
  2. Code type chunein: Barcode (Code128) ya QR code.
  3. Price on label chunein: Show price ya Hide price.
  4. Jin items ke labels chahiye unke saath Copies likhein.
  5. Preview dabayein, dekhein, phir Print dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Preview mein har copy ka aik label code aur (agar chuna ho) price ke saath nazar aata hai.

Zaroori noteBarcode scanner code ko POS ke search box mein type kar deta hai jis se item mil jata hai.

10. Inventory: stock ka control aur stock reports

Stock adjustments, movements ka log, expire hone wali batches aur stock valuation.

10.1 Stock Adjustment lagana

Maqsad: Jab purchase ya sale na ho tab stock badalta hai: mila hua maal, ginti ki durusti, nuqsan, samples.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Stock Adjustments → New Adjustment

Tareeqa:

  1. Item chunein.
  2. Type chunein: Stock In, Count Correction (Increase), Stock Out, Damage Write-off, Sample / Free Issue ya Count Correction (Decrease).
  3. Quantity (poora number) aur Date, aur chaahein to Reason likhein.
  4. Warehouse chunein.
  5. Batch-tracked item ke liye Batch chunein, ya aane wale stock ke liye New Batch No. aur Expiry Date likhein.
  6. Save Adjustment dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai stock quantity se barha ya kam hua; row table mein IN ya OUT badge ke saath nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Quantity must be a positive integer.Sifar se zyada poora number likhein.
Insufficient stock at this warehouse. Available: NWarehouse mein jitna hai us se zyada nikal nahi sakte.
Select which batch this adjustment applies to.Batch chunein.
Item, type, quantity and date are required.Bharein.
Zaroori noteHar adjustment item ki cost par value hoti hai aur accounts mein post hoti hai, isliye profit par asar daalti hai. Type imandari se chunein: ginti mein kami Count Correction hai, Damage nahi.

10.2 Stock Adjustment reverse karna

Maqsad: Ghalat adjustment ko wapas karta hai, stock bahal karta hai aur uski journal entry cancel karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Stock Adjustments → row ka Reverse button

Tareeqa:

  1. Table mein adjustment dhoondein.
  2. Reverse dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur item ka stock pehle jaisa ho jata hai.

Zaroori noteAgar reverse karne se stock manfi ho jaye to reversal radd ho sakta hai.

10.3 Stock Movements

Maqsad: Har stock tabdeeli ka log warehouse ke hisaab se: kab, item, warehouse, type, batch, miqdar, reference aur kis ne.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Stock Movements

Tareeqa:

  1. Inventory → Stock Movements kholein.
  2. Item, Warehouse aur Type se filter karke Filter dabayein.
  3. Print PDF ya Back to Stock Report istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Abhi kiya hua purchase ya sale movement ki surat mein nazar aata hai.

10.4 Expire hone wale aur expire shuda Batches

Maqsad: Batches ko unki expiry halat ke saath dikhata hai taake maal zaya hone se pehle kuch kiya ja sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Stock Valuation → Expiring Batches, ya Dashboard ke Near to Expiry / Expired Items buttons

Tareeqa:

  1. Page kholein.
  2. Warehouse, Business Category, Status (Expired, Expires Today, Near Expiry, OK, No Expiry Set) aur Near-expiry threshold (days) se filter karein.
  3. Filter dabayein; Clear reset karta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har row Days to Expiry, Unit Cost aur Stock Value dikhati hai.

10.5 Stock Valuation

Maqsad: Abhi stock mein kya hai aur uski cost: har item ka stock, reorder level, average cost, sale price, value aur status, aur reorder level se neeche items.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Stock Valuation

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Stock Valuation kholein.
  2. Chaahein to Warehouse, Business Category ya Item chunein; reset ke liye Clear.
  3. Batch-tracked item ke liye har batch dekhne ko batches dabayein.
  4. CSV, Excel ya Print istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Tamam warehouses chunne par total Balance Sheet ki Inventory line ke barabar hota hai.

Zaroori notePage stock ko abhi ki halat mein dikhata hai; is mein date range nahi.

11. Inventory: warehouse transfers

Stock ko aik warehouse se doosre mein bhejna. Menu mein sirf tab nazar aata hai jab aik se zyada warehouse hon.

11.1 Warehouse Transfers: list aur dekhna

Maqsad: Warehouses ke darmiyan stock ke transfers ki list dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Warehouse Transfers (sirf tab nazar aata hai jab aik se zyada warehouse hon)

Tareeqa:

  1. Page kholein.
  2. Status, From (Source) aur To (Destination) se filter karke Filter dabayein.
  3. Tafseel ke liye transfer par View dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har row Transfer #, Date, From, To, Items aur Status (Draft, Confirmed, Cancelled) dikhati hai.

11.2 Warehouse Transfer banana (Draft)

Maqsad: Aik warehouse se doosre mein bhejne wale items ki list tayyar karta hai. Abhi kuch nahi hilta.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Warehouse Transfers → New Transfer

Tareeqa:

  1. New Transfer dabayein.
  2. From (Source) aur To (Destination) chunein (alag hone chahiye) aur Date.
  3. Har item ke liye + Add Row dabayein; Item chunein, Quantity (poora number) likhein, aur batch-tracked item ke liye Batch (ya Auto rehne dein).
  4. Create Draft Transfer dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Transfer created as Draft. Confirm it to move stock.'

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Source and destination warehouse must be different.Do alag warehouses chunein.
A transfer needs at least one item.Row shamil karein.
Quantity must be a whole number.Ashariya hataein.

11.3 Warehouse Transfer confirm karna

Maqsad: Stock source warehouse se nikal kar destination mein daalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Warehouse Transfers → transfer kholein → Confirm Transfer

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft transfer ko View se kholein.
  2. Confirm Transfer dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai transfer confirm ho gaya — stock muntaqil hua; Stock Movements mein out aur in lines nazar aati hain.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Insufficient stock — ...Source warehouse mein kafi nahi; miqdar kam karein ya pehle stock daalein.
Zaroori noteTransfer aap ke apne warehouses ke darmiyan miqdar hilata hai; koi journal entry post nahi hoti.

11.4 Draft Transfer cancel karna

Maqsad: Aise transfer ko chhod deta hai jo confirm nahi hua.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transfer ke page par → Cancel Draft

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft transfer kholein.
  2. Cancel Draft dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Transfer cancelled.' aur status Cancelled hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Only a Draft transfer can be cancelled.Confirm shuda transfer ko reverse karna parta hai.

11.5 Confirm shuda Transfer reverse karna

Maqsad: Stock ko wapas wahin pahunchata hai jahan se aaya tha.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transfer ke page par → Reverse Transfer

Tareeqa:

  1. Confirm shuda transfer kholein.
  2. Reverse Transfer dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai transfer reverse ho gaya — stock bahal ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot reverse — ...Destination mein ab kafi nahi (bik gaya ya aagay chala gaya).
This transfer has already been reversed.Kuch karne ki zaroorat nahi.

12. Inventory: asli ginti (reconciliation)

Shelf par stock ginna aur system durust karna, doosre shakhs ki manzoori ke saath.

12.1 Inventory Reconciliation: list aur dekhna

Maqsad: Har warehouse ke liye asli ginti aur system stock ka muqabla dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Inventory Reconciliation

Tareeqa:

  1. Page kholein.
  2. Status aur Warehouse se filter karke Filter dabayein.
  3. Row par View dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Rows Reference, Warehouse, Date, Items, Net Variance aur Status (Draft, Counted, Approved, Posted, Cancelled) dikhati hain.

12.2 Asli ginti shuru karna (New Reconciliation)

Maqsad: Aik warehouse ke liye ginti sheet kholta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Inventory → Inventory Reconciliation → New Reconciliation

Tareeqa:

  1. New Reconciliation dabayein.
  2. Warehouse aur Count Date chunein.
  3. Count every active stock item at this warehouse (a full count) par tick karein, ya adhoori ginti ke liye khaas items chunein.
  4. Create Reconciliation dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai Draft ban gayi; aagay barhne ke liye asli ginti likhein.

12.3 Asli ginti likhna

Maqsad: Jo aap ne shelf par asal mein gina woh darj karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ka page (status Draft)

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft reconciliation kholein.
  2. Har item ki Physical Count (poora number) likhein, chaahein to Notes ke saath.
  3. Save Counts dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Counts saved.'

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... physical count must be a whole numberAshariya ya text hataein.
Only a Draft reconciliation accepts counts.Pehle Reopen for Recount karein.

12.4 Ginti finalize karna

Maqsad: Ginti ko lock karta hai aur us lamhe ki system miqdar likh leta hai taake farq nikala ja sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ke page par → Finalize Count

Tareeqa:

  1. Yaqeeni banayein ke har item ki ginti hai.
  2. Finalize Count dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Count finalized — system quantities snapshotted.' aur page System Qty, Physical Qty aur Variance dikhata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Every item needs a physical count before finalizingBaqi ginti likhein.

12.5 Ginti dobara karne ke liye reopen karna

Maqsad: Finalize shuda ginti ko Draft mein wapas laata hai taake durusti ho sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ke page par → Reopen for Recount

Tareeqa:

  1. Counted reconciliation kholein.
  2. Reopen for Recount dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Reopened for recount.' aur status Draft hota hai.

12.6 Reconciliation approve karna

Maqsad: Stock badalne se pehle doosra shakhs farq ki jaanch karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin, aur wohi nahi jis ne ginti ki.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ke page par → Approve

Tareeqa:

  1. Counted reconciliation kholein.
  2. Har item ka Variance dekhein.
  3. Approve dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Reconciliation approved.'

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
The person who counted this reconciliation cannot also approve it.Kisi doosre admin se kahein.

12.7 Reconciliation post karna (farq lagoo karna)

Maqsad: Stock ko ginti ki miqdar par laata hai aur qeemat ka farq accounts mein post karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ke page par → Post — Apply Variances

Tareeqa:

  1. Approved reconciliation kholein.
  2. Post — Apply Variances dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai reconciliation post ho gayi — stock adjust hua; Stock Movements mein tabdeeliyan nazar aati hain.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot post — system stock changed since counting ...Ginti ke baad stock hila; reopen kar ke dobara gintein.
Zaroori notePost karne ke baad cancel nahi ho sakta. Aik journal entry khalis farq ko Stock Adjustment ke muqable record karti hai.

12.8 Reconciliation cancel karna

Maqsad: Aisi ginti chhod deta hai jo post nahi hui. Stock nahi hilta.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reconciliation ke page par → Cancel

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft ya Counted reconciliation kholein.
  2. Cancel dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai 'Reconciliation cancelled.'

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... cannot be cancelledApproved ya Posted reconciliation is tareeqe se cancel nahi hoti.

13. Kharidari: purchases aur suppliers ko payments

Order se adaigi tak: purchase orders, purchase invoices, supplier payments aur purchase returns.

13.1 Purchases dekhna aur dhoondna

Maqsad: Purchase invoices ko supplier, items, miqdar, net total, paid, due, status aur date ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases

Tareeqa:

  1. Transactions → Purchases kholein.
  2. Supplier ke naam ka hissa likhein aur Search dabayein; reset ke liye Clear.
  3. Status column parhein: draft, ya payment status Unpaid, Partial ya Paid (reverse ho to reversed badge ke saath).
  4. Print PDF, ya payments ke liye Supplier Payments istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Due, Net Total minus Paid ke barabar hota hai.

13.2 Purchase banana (Draft)

Maqsad: Supplier se kharide gaye maal ko darj karta hai. Draft ki surat mein save hota hai; post karne tak kuch book nahi hota.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → New Purchase Invoice

Tareeqa:

  1. Select Supplier dabayein aur supplier chunein.
  2. Date dekhein aur Received Into (Warehouse) chunein. Chaahein to Notes likhein.
  3. Har item ke liye: Select Item… dabayein; agar item ki aik se zyada unit hon to Unit chunein; Qty aur Price / Unit likhein; chaahein to Discount (% ya Fixed) aur Tax (%).
  4. Batch-tracked item ke liye Batch No aur Expiry Date bharein.
  5. Mazeed lines ke liye Add Item dabayein (× line hata deta hai).
  6. Save Purchase dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Draft Purchase #N saved.' likhta hai aur row par draft hota hai. Stock aur supplier ka ledger abhi nahi badle.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Supplier and date are required!Supplier aur date chunein.
At least one item is required!Item aur miqdar wali line shamil karein.
Access denied. Only managers and admins can add purchases.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteYahan di gayi qeematen aur raqmein sirf form samajhane ki misalen hain; jab tak aap save na karein koi asli purchase nahi hota.

13.3 Draft Purchase edit karna

Maqsad: Post karne se pehle purchase ki durusti karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → pencil button (Edit purchase)

Tareeqa:

  1. draft row par pencil button dabayein.
  2. Supplier, date, notes ya lines badlein.
  3. Update Purchase dabayein (ya Cancel).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai purchase kamyabi se update ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Purchase #N is posted to the general ledger and can no longer be changed. Reverse it instead.Post shuda purchase edit nahi hota: admin Correct ya Reverse kar sakta hai.
Supplier and date are required!Bharein.
At least one item is required!Aik line rakhein.

13.4 Purchase post karna

Maqsad: Draft ko asli purchase banata hai: stock barhta hai, invoice number lagta hai, supplier ledger aur books update hoti hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → draft row par hara tick (Post)

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft durust hone ki tasdeeq karein (shak ho to Edit purchase kholein).
  2. Hare tick button ko dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Purchase <invoice number> posted successfully!' likhta hai; row ko invoice number aur payment status milta hai; chune hue warehouse mein item ka stock barh jata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Purchase #N is not a Draft — nothing to post.Ye pehle hi post ho chuka hai.
Zaroori notePost ke baad edit ya delete nahi ho sakta; sirf admin correct ya reverse kar sakta hai. Stock ki qeemat tax ke baghair maal ki cost par hoti hai.

13.5 Draft Purchase delete karna

Maqsad: Gair-zaroori draft phenk deta hai. Is ka stock ya accounts par koi asar nahi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → draft row ka trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Delete this Draft Purchase?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai purchase kamyabi se delete ho gaya.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete purchase with associated payments! Delete payments first.Pehle payments reverse karein.
Cannot delete purchase — it was created from Purchase Order #N.Rehne dein, ya post shuda ho to reverse karein.
Zaroori notePost shuda ya number lagi purchase kabhi delete nahi hoti; reverse karein.

13.6 Purchase Invoice dekhna aur print karna

Maqsad: Invoice ko printable page ki surat mein kholta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → receipt button (View invoice)

Tareeqa:

  1. Row par View invoice dabayein; ye naye tab mein khulta hai.
  2. Hare print button ko dabayein, phir browser window mein print ya PDF save karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Invoice mein supplier, lines aur totals hote hain. Reverse shuda purchase par REVERSED watermark aata hai.

13.7 Post shuda Purchase durust karna (Correct)

Maqsad: Post shuda purchase ki ghalti aik hi qadam mein theek karta hai, reverse aur dobara darj karne ke bajaye.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → amber shield button (Correct this Posted Purchase)

Tareeqa:

  1. Post shuda row par shield button dabayein.
  2. Original Purchase ko Corrected Purchase Details se milayein; supplier, notes ya lines badlein.
  3. Reason for correction likhein.
  4. Agar purchase par active payments hon to tasdeeq par tick karein ke wo jaisi hain waisi rahengi.
  5. Apply Correction dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai purchase kamyabi se durust ho gaya; audit log mein wajah likhi hoti hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
A reason for this correction is required.Wajah likhein.
Cannot correct — insufficient stock to undo the original receipt: ...Maal ka kuch hissa bik chuka; pehle sale correct ya reverse karein.
... has N Purchase Return(s) recordedReturns wali purchase correct nahi hoti; pehle returns reverse karein.
Zaroori noteMaujooda payments jaisi hain waisi rehti hain; agar durust shuda total adaigi se kam ho to farq supplier credit ban jata hai.

13.8 Purchase reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Purchase ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchases → post shuda row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Purchase dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Purchase journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteStock wapas kam hota hai, supplier ledger durust hota hai aur cancelling journal entry post hoti hai. Agar purchase par active payments hon to ziyada warning milti hai aur tasdeeq karni parti hai; payments record mein rehti hain aur supplier credit ban sakta hai. Agar maal bik chuka ho to reversal radd ho sakta hai.

13.9 Purchase Order banana

Maqsad: Supplier ko diya gaya order darj karta hai. Ye stock nahi hilata aur kuch post nahi karta.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchase Orders → New Purchase Order

Tareeqa:

  1. Supplier aur Order Date chunein; chaahein to Expected Delivery aur Notes.
  2. Har item ke liye + Add Row dabayein; item, unit, Qty, Purchase Price, discount aur tax chunein.
  3. Create PO dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Purchase Order #N created.' likhta hai aur wo status ke saath list mein nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Supplier and order date are required.Dono bharein.
At least one item is required.Row shamil karein.

13.10 Purchase Order dekhna aur status badalna

Maqsad: Order ko Draft, Confirmed, Received ya Cancelled se guzarte hue track karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchase Orders → View

Tareeqa:

  1. Order par View dabayein.
  2. status box mein naya status chunein aur Update Status dabayein.
  3. Print PDF ya Back istemal karein.
