Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'PO #N status updated to ...' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot change status of a Received or Cancelled order.Ye dono aakhri halatein hain.

13.11 Purchase Order ko Purchase mein badalna

Maqsad: Maal aane par order ko asli, post shuda purchase mein badalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Purchase order ke page par → Convert to Purchase

Tareeqa:

  1. Order kholein.
  2. Button ke saath wali date field mein purchase_date set karein.
  3. Convert to Purchase dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai PO kamyabi se Purchase <invoice number> mein badal gaya; order Received dikhata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cancelled orders cannot be converted.Naya order banayein.
Already converted to Purchase #N.Wo purchase kholein.
PO has no line items.Pehle items shamil karein.
Zaroori notePurchase pehle se post shuda banta hai, isliye stock aur supplier ledger foran badal jate hain. Page par warehouse box nahi, isliye maal default warehouse mein jata hai.

13.12 Purchase Order delete karna

Maqsad: Gair-zaroori order hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Purchase order ke page par → Delete PO

Tareeqa:

  1. Order kholein.
  2. Delete PO dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Purchase Order #N deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete a received order.Received order record ke tor par rehta hai.

13.13 Supplier ko Payment darj karna

Maqsad: Supplier ko di gayi raqam darj karta hai, chaahein to kisi khaas purchase ke muqable.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Supplier Payments → Record Payment

Tareeqa:

  1. Select Supplier dabayein aur supplier chunein.
  2. Chaahein to Against Purchase chunein, ya General / On Account rehne dein.
  3. Amount aur Payment Date likhein.
  4. Method aur wo Account chunein jis se raqam gayi.
  5. Chaahein to Reference No. aur Notes likhein.
  6. Save Payment dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai payment kamyabi se darj ho gayi; wo table mein nazar aati hai; supplier ka balance kam hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Supplier, amount and payment date are required!Bharein.
Payment exceeds purchase balance due (...)!Us purchase par jitna dena hai us se zyada na dein.
Selected purchase is still a Draft and has no payable balance yet — post it first.Pehle purchase post karein.
Invalid payment method!Cash, Bank, Cheque, Online mein se aik chunein.
Zaroori noteSahi Account chunein: yehi tay karta hai ke kaunsa cash ya bank balance kam hoga aur Cash Flow aur Balance Sheet kya dikhayen ge.

13.14 Supplier Payment edit karna

Halat: Payment save hote hi books mein post ho jati hai, aur code post shuda payment ko badalne nahi deta. Isliye Edit dabane par 'Payment #N is posted to the general ledger and can no longer be changed. Reverse it instead.' aane ki umeed hai (code se parha, browser mein nahi azmaya).

Maqsad: List mein har payment par Edit button hota hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Supplier Payments → pencil button (Edit)

Tareeqa:

  1. Payment durust karne ke liye: use Reverse karein (agla box), phir sahi tarah dobara darj karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Durust shuda payment list mein nazar aati hai; ghalat wali reversed dikhti hai.

13.15 Supplier Payment reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Supplier Payment ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Supplier Payments → row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Supplier Payment dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Supplier Payment journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori notePayment cancel hoti hai, supplier ka balance wapas barhta hai aur cash ya bank balance bahal hota hai.

13.16 Kai Purchases ki aik saath Payment (Bulk Payment)

Maqsad: Supplier ke khule bills aik saath ada karta hai, chaahein to kul raqam khud taqseem hoti hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Bulk Payment (ya Supplier Payments page par Bulk Payment)

Tareeqa:

  1. Supplier chunein.
  2. Chaahein to Total Payment Amount likhein aur Load & Auto-Distribute dabayein; ya har bill ka balance bharne ke liye Pay Full Balance.
  3. Har bill ke saamne Pay Now theek karein (PUR #, Date, Invoice Total, Already Paid, Balance Due); general box baqi raqam on account leta hai.
  4. Payment Date, Payment Method, Account, chaahein to Reference / Cheque # aur Notes likhein.
  5. Save Bulk Payment dabayein (Clear All boxes khali karta hai).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Supplier Payments page par har bill ki aik payment nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Please enter at least one payment amount.Kam az kam aik Pay Now box bharein.
Supplier and payment date are required!Dono chunein.

13.17 Purchase Return darj karna

Maqsad: Asal purchase line ke muqable maal supplier ko wapas bhejta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchase Returns

Tareeqa:

  1. Transactions → Purchase Returns kholein aur Record Purchase Return dhoondein.
  2. Return Date chunein.
  3. Har line ke liye — Select Purchase / Item — dabayein aur purchase line chunein; Qty, Return Price aur chaahein to Reason likhein.
  4. Mazeed lines ke liye Add Item dabayein.
  5. Record Returns dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'N purchase return(s) recorded successfully!' likhta hai; returns table unhein dikhata hai aur stock kam hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Row N (...): cannot return X, only Y remaining.Jitna kharida us se, pehle wapas kiye gaye ko minus kar ke, zyada wapas nahi kar sakte.
Row N: quantity must be a positive integer.Sifar se zyada poore number.
Row N: return price cannot be negative.Sifar ya zyada likhein.
Date is required!Date chunein.
Zaroori noteReturn tab istemal karein jab maal asal mein wapas gaya ho. Reversal tab jab document hi ghalat tha.

13.18 Purchase Return reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Purchase Return ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Purchase Returns → row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Purchase Return dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Purchase Return journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteReturn cancel hota hai aur uske stock aur ledger ke asraat wapas ho jate hain.

14. Farokht: sales aur customers se receipts

Quotations, sales invoices, delivery challans, customer receipts aur sale returns. Counter POS ka alag hissa hai.

14.1 Sales dekhna aur dhoondna

Maqsad: Sales invoices ko customer, items, miqdar, net total, received, due, status aur date ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales

Tareeqa:

  1. Transactions → Sales kholein.
  2. Customer ke naam se search karein; Clear reset karta hai.
  3. Status parhein: draft, ya Unpaid, Partial ya Paid.
  4. Print PDF ya Customer Receipts istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Due, Net Total minus Received ke barabar hota hai.

14.2 Sale banana (Draft)

Maqsad: Customer ko becha gaya maal darj karta hai. Draft ki surat mein save hota hai; stock aur accounts tab badalte hain jab aap post karein.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → New Sale Invoice

Tareeqa:

  1. Select Customer dabayein aur customer chunein.
  2. Date dekhein, wo Warehouse chunein jahan se maal jayega, aur chaahein to Notes likhein.
  3. Har item ke liye Select Item… dabayein; Unit chunein; Qty aur Sale Price likhein; Stock column dekhte rahein; chaahein to Discount (% ya Fixed) aur Tax (%).
  4. Mazeed lines ke liye Add Item dabayein (× aik line hata deta hai).
  5. Save Sale dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Draft Sale #N saved.' likhta hai aur row par draft hota hai; stock abhi nahi hila.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Customer and date are required!Dono chunein.
At least one item is required!Line shamil karein.
Insufficient stock — ...Chune hue warehouse mein aap ki likhi miqdar se kam hai; miqdar kam karein ya doosra warehouse chunein.
Access denied. Only managers and admins can add sales.Manager ya admin se kahein.
Zaroori noteWarehouse mein jitna hai us se zyada nahi bech sakte. Is manual ki raqmein sirf misalen hain.

14.3 Draft Sale edit karna

Maqsad: Post karne se pehle sale ki durusti karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → draft row ka pencil button (Edit sale)

Tareeqa:

  1. Pencil button dabayein.
  2. Customer, date, notes ya lines badlein.
  3. Update Sale dabayein (ya Cancel).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai sale kamyabi se update ho gayi.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Sale #N is posted to the general ledger and can no longer be changed. Reverse it instead.Post shuda sale edit nahi hoti; admin Correct ya Reverse kar sakta hai.
Insufficient stock — ...Miqdar kam karein.

14.4 Sale post karna

Maqsad: Draft ko asli sale banata hai: stock kam hota hai (batches pehle expire hone wale pehle), invoice number lagta hai, customer ledger aur books update hoti hain, aur bechay gaye maal ki cost record hoti hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → draft row par hara tick (Post)

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft check karein.
  2. Hare tick button ko dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Sale <invoice number> posted successfully!' likhta hai; row ko invoice number aur payment status milta hai; stock kam hua.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot post — insufficient stock: ...Draft save karne ke baad stock badal gaya; miqdar kam karein ya stock lein.
Sale #N is not a Draft — nothing to post.Pehle hi post ho chuki.
Zaroori notePost ke baad edit ya delete nahi ho sakti; sirf admin correct ya reverse kar sakta hai.

14.5 Draft Sale delete karna

Maqsad: Gair-zaroori draft chhod deta hai. Stock ya accounts par koi asar nahi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → draft row ka trash button

Tareeqa:

  1. Trash button dabayein.
  2. 'Delete this Draft Sale?' ki tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai sale kamyabi se delete ho gayi.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete sale with associated receipts! Delete receipts first.Pehle receipts reverse karein.
Cannot delete sale — it was created from Quotation #N.Rehne dein.

14.6 Sale Invoice dekhna aur print karna

Maqsad: Invoice ko printable page ki surat mein kholta hai jo customer ko diya ja sake.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → receipt button (View invoice)

Tareeqa:

  1. View invoice dabayein; ye naye tab mein khulta hai.
  2. Hare print button ko dabayein aur print ya PDF save karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Invoice mein customer, lines aur totals hote hain; reverse shuda sale par REVERSED watermark aata hai.

14.7 Post shuda Sale durust karna (Correct)

Maqsad: Post shuda sale ki ghalti aik hi qadam mein theek karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → amber shield button (Correct this Posted Sale)

Tareeqa:

  1. Post shuda row par shield button dabayein.
  2. Original Sale ko Corrected Sale Details se milayein aur customer, notes ya lines badlein.
  3. Reason for correction likhein.
  4. Agar sale par receipts hon to tasdeeq par tick karein ke wo jaisi hain waisi rahengi aur kami customer credit banegi.
  5. Apply Correction dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai sale kamyabi se durust ho gayi.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
A reason for this correction is required.Wajah likhein.
Cannot correct — insufficient stock: ...Durust shuda lines ke liye stock kafi nahi.
... has N Sale Return(s) recorded against itPehle returns reverse karein.

14.8 Sale reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Sale ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sales → post shuda row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Sale dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Sale journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteStock wapas barhta hai, customer ledger durust hota hai aur cancelling journal entry post hoti hai. Agar sale par receipts hon to ziyada warning milti hai aur tasdeeq karni parti hai; receipts rehti hain aur customer credit ban sakta hai.

14.9 Quotation banana

Maqsad: Customer ke liye qeemat ki pesh-kash tayyar karta hai. Ye stock nahi hilata aur kuch post nahi karta.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Quotations → New Quotation

Tareeqa:

  1. Customer aur Quote Date chunein; chaahein to Valid Until aur Notes.
  2. Har item ke liye + Add Row dabayein; item, unit, Qty, Sale Price, discount type aur value, aur tax % chunein.
  3. Create Quotation dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Quotation #N created.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Customer and date are required.Dono bharein.
At least one item is required.Row shamil karein.

14.10 Quotation edit karna

Maqsad: Convert hone se pehle quotation badalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Quotation ke page par → Edit

Tareeqa:

  1. Quotation ko View se kholein, phir Edit dabayein.
  2. Fields ya rows badlein.
  3. Save Changes dabayein (ya Cancel).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Quotation #N updated.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Converted quotations cannot be edited.Agar banne wali sale abhi draft ho to use edit karein.

14.11 Quotation ka status badalna

Maqsad: Quotation ko Draft, Sent, Accepted ya Rejected ke taur par track karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Quotation ke page par → status box → Update

Tareeqa:

  1. Quotation kholein.
  2. Status chunein aur Update dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Quotation #N marked as ...' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Converted quotations cannot be changed.Convert shuda quotation aakhri hoti hai.

14.12 Quotation ko Sale mein badalna

Maqsad: Manzoor shuda quotation ko asli, post shuda sale mein badalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Quotation ke page par → Convert to Sale

Tareeqa:

  1. Quotation kholein.
  2. sale_date set karein aur Warehouse (location) chunein.
  3. Convert to Sale dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai quotation Sale <invoice number> mein badal gayi; status Converted hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot convert a rejected or already-converted quotation.Nayi quotation banayein.
Insufficient stock — ...Warehouse mein kisi item ki miqdar kam hai.
Already converted to Sale #N.Wo sale kholein.
Zaroori noteSale pehle se post shuda banti hai, isliye stock, customer ledger aur books foran badal jate hain.

14.13 Quotation delete karna

Maqsad: Gair-zaroori quotation hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Quotation ke page par → Delete

Tareeqa:

  1. Quotation kholein.
  2. Delete dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Quotation #N deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cannot delete a converted quotation.Ye sale se juri hui hai.

14.14 Delivery Challan banana

Maqsad: Invoice shuda sale ki rawangi darj karta hai. Ye kuch post nahi karta aur stock nahi hilata.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin banate hain; koi bhi verified user list dekh sakta hai.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Delivery Challans

Tareeqa:

  1. Transactions → Delivery Challans kholein.
  2. List se Sale Invoice aur Challan Date chunein.
  3. Chaahein to Transport / Carrier aur Notes likhein.
  4. Create Challan dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Delivery Challan #N created.' likhta hai aur wo Pending status ke saath nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Sale and challan date are required.Dono bharein.
A challan already exists for this sale.Maujooda ko update karein.

14.15 Delivery Challan update karna

Maqsad: Challan ko Pending, Dispatched, Delivered ya Cancelled ke taur par track karta hai aur dates aur transport likhta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Delivery Challans → Update column

Tareeqa:

  1. Challan ka update panel kholein (Update DC #N).
  2. Status, Transport, Dispatch Date, Delivery Date ya notes badlein.
  3. Save dabayein (ya Cancel).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Challan #N updated.' likhta hai.

14.16 Customer se Receipt darj karna

Maqsad: Customer se mili raqam darj karta hai, chaahein to kisi khaas sale ke muqable.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Customer Receipts → Record Receipt

Tareeqa:

  1. Select Customer dabayein aur customer chunein.
  2. Chaahein to Against Sale chunein, ya General / On Account rehne dein.
  3. Amount aur Receipt Date likhein.
  4. Method aur wo Account chunein jis mein raqam gayi.
  5. Chaahein to Reference No. aur Notes likhein.
  6. Save Receipt dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai receipt kamyabi se darj ho gayi; customer ka balance kam hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Customer, amount and receipt date are required!Bharein.
Receipt exceeds sale balance due (...)!Us sale par jitna baqi hai us se zyada na lein.
Selected sale is still a Draft and has no receivable balance yet — post it first.Pehle sale post karein.
Invalid payment method!Cash, Bank, Cheque ya Online chunein.

14.17 Customer Receipt edit karna

Halat: Receipt save hote hi books mein post ho jati hai, aur code post shuda receipt ko badalne nahi deta, isliye Edit par 'Payment #N is posted to the general ledger and can no longer be changed. Reverse it instead.' aane ki umeed hai (code se parha, browser mein nahi azmaya).

Maqsad: List mein har receipt par Edit button hota hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Customer Receipts → pencil button (Edit)

Tareeqa:

  1. Receipt durust karne ke liye: use Reverse karein (agla box), phir sahi tarah dobara darj karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Durust receipt nazar aati hai; ghalat wali reversed dikhti hai.

14.18 Customer Receipt reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Customer Receipt ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Customer Receipts → row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Customer Receipt dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Customer Receipt journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteReceipt cancel hoti hai, customer ka balance wapas barhta hai aur cash ya bank balance kam hota hai.

14.19 Kai Sales par aik saath Receipt (Bulk Receipt)

Maqsad: Customer ki adaigi kai khule invoices par darj karta hai, chaahein to kul raqam khud taqseem hoti hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Bulk Receipt (ya Customer Receipts page par Bulk Receipt)

Tareeqa:

  1. Customer chunein.
  2. Chaahein to Total Receipt Amount likhein aur Load & Auto-Distribute dabayein, ya Receive Full Balance.
  3. Har invoice ke saamne Receive Now theek karein (SAL #, Date, Invoice Total, Already Received, Balance Due); general box baqi raqam on account leta hai.
  4. Receipt Date, Payment Method, Account, chaahein to Reference / Cheque # aur Notes likhein.
  5. Save Bulk Receipt dabayein (Clear All boxes khali karta hai).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Customer Receipts page par har invoice ki aik receipt nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Please enter at least one receipt amount.Kam az kam aik Receive Now box bharein.
Customer and receipt date are required!Dono chunein.

14.20 Sale Return darj karna

Maqsad: Asal sale line ke muqable customer se maal wapas leta hai, usi cost par jis par gaya tha.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sale Returns

Tareeqa:

  1. Transactions → Sale Returns kholein aur Record Sale Return dhoondein.
  2. Return Date chunein.
  3. Har line ke liye — Select Sale / Item — dabayein aur sale line chunein; Qty, Return Price aur chaahein to Reason likhein.
  4. Mazeed lines ke liye Add Item dabayein.
  5. Record Returns dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'N sale return(s) recorded successfully!' likhta hai; stock barhta hai aur customer ka balance kam hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Row N (...): cannot return X, only Y remaining.Jitna becha us se, pehle wapas kiye gaye ko minus kar ke, zyada wapas nahi le sakte.
Row N: quantity must be a positive integer.Sifar se zyada poore number.
Date is required!Date chunein.
Zaroori noteDraft ya reverse shuda sale par return nahi hota.

14.21 Sale Return reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Sale Return ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Sale Returns → row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Sale Return dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Sale Return journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteReturn cancel hota hai aur uske stock aur ledger ke asraat wapas ho jate hain.

15. Point of Sale (POS)

Manager aur admin ke liye tez counter screen. Aam sale ke bar-aks, POS sale foran post hoti hai.

15.1 POS Sale karna

Maqsad: Counter par items bechta hai, payment leta hai, aur sale foran post karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Menu mein POS

Tareeqa:

  1. POS kholein aur wo Warehouse dekhein jahan se maal jayega.
  2. Search box (Scan barcode / QR, or type an item name) par click karein, scan ya type karke item shamil karein; har item ke liye dohrayein.
  3. Zaroorat ho to doosra customer chunne ke liye Walk-in Customer dabayein.
  4. Us cash ya bank account ka Payment method button dabayein jis mein raqam jayegi.
  5. Amount received likhein; Change line batati hai kitni wapas deni hai.
  6. Complete Sale dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Receipt print ke liye khulti hai; chune hue warehouse mein stock kam hua; sale Sales mein invoice number ke saath nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
The cart is empty.Pehle item shamil karein.
Only N × <item> available ...Us warehouse mein cart se kam hai.
Invalid amount paid.Number likhein.
Bad quantity or price for <item>.Us cart line ko theek karein.
Zaroori noteAgar aap kul se kam raqam likhein to mili hui raqam record hoti hai aur baqi customer par baqaya rehta hai — Walk-in Customer par bhi — isliye naqd sale mein poori raqam likhein. Clear Cart bina bechay screen khali karta hai.

15.2 POS par Batch chunna

Maqsad: Batch-tracked items ke liye cashier ko batch chunne deta hai, khud-kaar sab se pehle expire hone wali batch ke bajaye.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: POS → cart mein batch-tracked item ke neeche Batch: Auto (FEFO) link

Tareeqa:

  1. Batch-tracked item cart mein shamil karein.
  2. Uske neeche Batch: Auto (FEFO) dabayein.
  3. List se batch(es) aur miqdar chunein; expired ya expiry ke qareeb batches par nishan hota hai.
  4. Sale jari rakhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Cart line par Auto (FEFO) ki jagah 'Batch: <number>' nazar aata hai.

Zaroori noteAgar aap kuch na karein to system us batch se stock leta hai jo pehle expire hoti hai.

15.3 Bill Hold karna

Maqsad: Mojooda cart ko alag rakhta hai, maslan jab kisi aur customer ko dekhna ho.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: POS → Hold Bill

Tareeqa:

  1. Cart banayein.
  2. Hold Bill dabayein.
  3. Cart saaf ho jati hai aur bill hold number ke saath save ho jata hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Bill Held Bills mein nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Cart is empty.Pehle items shamil karein.
Customer required.Customer chunein (Walk-in Customer bhi chalta hai).
Hold was updated by another user. Please reload and try again.Held bill dobara load karein.
Zaroori noteHeld bill sale nahi hota: jab tak aap mukammal na karein stock nahi hilta aur kuch post nahi hota.

15.4 Held Bill dobara chalana

Maqsad: Held bill ko POS cart mein wapas laata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Menu mein Held Bills (ya POS screen par Held Bills)

Tareeqa:

  1. Held Bills kholein.
  2. Customer ya hold number se search karein, ya Most Recent, Customer Name ya Total Amount se sort karein.
  3. Bill par Resume dabayein.
  4. Complete Sale se mukammal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: POS cart mein wohi customer aur items nazar aate hain.

15.5 Held Bill delete karna

Maqsad: Aise held bill ko hata deta hai jo istemal nahi hoga.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Held Bills → Delete

Tareeqa:

  1. Held Bills kholein.
  2. Bill par Delete dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Bill list se ghayab ho jata hai.

15.6 POS Receipt print karna

Maqsad: Mukammal POS sale ki receipt 80 mm kaghaz par print karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Complete Sale ke baad khulti hai; Print button receipt page par hota hai.

Tareeqa:

  1. Complete Sale ke baad receipt page khulta hai.
  2. Print dabayein aur receipt printer chunein.
  3. Back to POS counter par wapas le jata hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: 80 mm ki tang parchi items, totals aur (jahan set ho) barcode ke saath print hoti hai.

Zaroori noteSale page ke address ka aakhir /pos/receipt/<number> hota hai; ?format=legacy lagane se purana chaura layout nazar aata hai.

16. Akhrajaat (Expenses)

Rozmarra ke akhrajaat jaise kiraya aur bijli.

16.1 Expense darj karna

Maqsad: Karobar chalane par kharch ki gayi raqam darj karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Expenses

Tareeqa:

  1. Description likhein (misal: Office Rent).
  2. Amount aur Date likhein.
  3. Category chunein (ye tay karti hai ke kharch kis account mein jayega).
  4. Payment Method aur wo Account chunein jis se raqam gayi.
  5. Chaahein to Reference # aur Notes likhein.
  6. Save Expense dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Expense recorded.' likhta hai aur row table mein nazar aati hai; Profit & Loss mein shamil hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Description, amount and date are required.Bharein.
Amount must be positive.Sifar se zyada likhein.
Invalid payment method!Chaaron mein se aik chunein.
Invalid amount or date.Format durust karein.
Zaroori noteSahi Account chunein; yehi tay karta hai ke kaunsa cash ya bank balance kam hoga.

16.2 Expense Category shamil karna

Maqsad: Category banata hai aur chunta hai ke wo kis ledger account mein post hogi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Expenses → category box (New category name)

Tareeqa:

  1. New category name likhein.
  2. Uske saath wale box mein ledger account chunein (default 6090 Other Expenses hai).
  3. Add dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Category '...' added.' likhta hai aur expense form ki Category list mein nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Category name is required.Naam likhein.
Category already exists.Maujooda istemal karein.

16.3 Expense reverse karna

Maqsad: Post shuda Expense ko cancel karta hai jo nahi hona chahiye tha, asal aur cancelling dono entries record mein rakhte hue.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Transactions → Expenses → row par Reverse

Tareeqa:

  1. List mein Expense dhoondein.
  2. Row par Reverse dabayein.
  3. Tasdeeqi message parhein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Row par reversed badge aata hai aur message batata hai ke Expense journal entry number ke saath reverse ho gaya.

Zaroori noteExpense cancel hota hai aur cash ya bank balance bahal hota hai. Expenses page par edit button nahi: expense durust karne ke liye reverse karein aur dobara darj karein.

17. Accounting (Hisaab-kitaab)

Chart of accounts, cash aur bank balances, journal entries, fixed assets, tax codes aur fiscal years. Menu: Accounting.

17.1 Chart of Accounts dekhna

Maqsad: Har ledger account ko code, type, flags aur balance ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Chart of Accounts

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Chart of Accounts kholein.
  2. Balances as of mein date chunein aur us din ke balances dekhne ke liye Show dabayein.
  3. Print PDF, ya Tax Codes aur Dashboard buttons istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Jin accounts mein system post karta hai un par system flag hota hai.

17.2 Ledger Account shamil karna

Maqsad: Naya account shamil karta hai, maslan naya kharch.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Chart of Accounts → New Account

Tareeqa:

  1. New Account dabayein.
  2. Code aur Name likhein (misal: 6050, Internet & Broadband).
  3. Chaahein to Parent (main account) chunein.
  4. Type chunein.
  5. Chaahein to Cash flow section set karein.
  6. This is a main account (a heading) par sirf tab tick karein jab wo aisa heading ho jis mein kuch post nahi hota.
  7. Create Account dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Account <code> — <name> created.' likhta hai aur wo chart mein nazar aata hai.

17.3 Ledger Account edit karna

Maqsad: Account ka naam badalta hai, use kisi aur ke neeche le jata hai, ya inactive karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Chart of Accounts → pencil button (Edit)

Tareeqa:

  1. Pencil button dabayein.
  2. Name, Parent, Cash flow section ya Active tick badlein.
  3. Save dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Account <code> — <name> updated.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
An account cannot be its own parent.Koi doosra parent chunein.
This account has journal entries; it cannot be deactivated.Active rehne dein.
This heading still has active sub-accounts.Pehle sub-accounts inactive karein.

17.4 Ledger Account delete karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se bane aur kabhi istemal na hone wale account ko hata deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Chart of Accounts → Edit → Delete this account

Tareeqa:

  1. Account ko pencil button se kholein.
  2. Delete this account dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Account <code> — <name> deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... is used by the posting layer and cannot be deleted.System account hai; chhor dein.
... has journal entries. Deactivate it instead.Edit kar ke Active ka tick hataein.
... still has sub-accounts.Pehle unhein hataein ya muntaqil karein.
... belongs to a cash/bank account / is used by an expense category / is used by a tax code.Pehle wo link badlein.

17.5 Tax Codes: dekhna aur rates badalna

Maqsad: Har tax component ko rate aur input/output accounts ke saath dikhata hai, aur rates badalne deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Tax Codes (Chart of Accounts par bhi button hai)

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Tax Codes kholein.
  2. Rate % box mein figure badlein.
  3. Save Rates dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Tax rates updated.' likhta hai.

Zaroori noteTax rates sirf admin badal sakta hai. Tax wale documents post karne se pehle rates dekh lein.

17.6 Fiscal Years aur Periods dekhna

Maqsad: Har fiscal year aur uske mahine open ya closed status ke saath dikhata hai. Open period ke bahar ki date par kuch post nahi ho sakta.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fiscal Years & Periods

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Fiscal Years & Periods kholein.
  2. Period, From, To, Status columns parhein.
  3. Print PDF istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aaj wala mahina list mein hota hai aur open hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
No fiscal year covers <date>. Create the fiscal year first.Admin ko fiscal year banana hoga.
<period> is closed. Post to an open period.Open mahine ki date istemal karein, ya admin se reopen karwayein.

17.7 Fiscal Year banana

Maqsad: Aik saal aur uske barah mahine ke periods banata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fiscal Years & Periods → Create fiscal year

Tareeqa:

  1. Saal ka number likhein (misal: 2027).
  2. Create dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Fiscal year <year> created with 12 periods.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Enter a valid year.Chaar-ankon wala saal likhein.
Fiscal year <year> already exists.Kuch karne ki zaroorat nahi.

17.8 Period close ya reopen karna

Maqsad: Mahine ko lock karta hai taake uske figures na badlein, ya unlock karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fiscal Years & Periods → Action column mein Close / Reopen

Tareeqa:

  1. Mahina dhoondein.
  2. Close dabayein (band mahine ke liye Reopen).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message '<period> closed.' ya '<period> reopened.' likhta hai aur Status badal jata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Fiscal year ... is closed; its periods cannot be reopened individually.Saal band hone ke baad uske mahine band hi rehte hain.

17.9 Fiscal Year close karna

Maqsad: Saal ka nafa ya nuqsan Retained Earnings mein muntaqil karta hai aur us saal ke har period ko lock karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fiscal Years & Periods → Close Year

Tareeqa:

  1. Month End ke checks mukammal karein (Month End dekhein).
  2. Backup download karein.
  3. Close Year dabayein aur tasdeeq parhein.
  4. Tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message batata hai saal band ho gaya aur kitna nafa ya nuqsan Retained Earnings mein gaya.

Zaroori notePage se ye wapas nahi ho sakta. Naye saal mein invoice numbering dobara shuru hoti hai.

17.10 Cash aur Bank Accounts: balances dekhna

Maqsad: Har cash ya bank account ko opening aur current balance ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Cash & Bank Accounts

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Cash & Bank Accounts kholein.
  2. Account, Type, Opening aur Current Balance parhein.
  3. Account add ya edit karne ke liye Manage Accounts (admin); print ke liye Print PDF.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Balances Balance Sheet ki cash aur bank lines ke barabar hote hain.

Zaroori noteBalance hamesha general ledger se parha jata hai, isliye us account mein post hui har journal entry shamil hoti hai, sirf receipts, payments aur expenses nahi.

17.11 Cash aur Bank Account Ledger

Maqsad: Aik account ki har harkat dikhata hai: date, description, in, out aur balance.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Cash & Bank Accounts → row ka ledger button

Tareeqa:

  1. Account ka ledger button dabayein.
  2. Lines parhein; wapas jane ke liye Back to Accounts dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aakhri Balance account ke Current Balance ke barabar hota hai.

17.12 Journal Entries dekhna

Maqsad: Haliya journal entries aur har account ke debit aur credit ka khulasa dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Journal Entries

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Journal Entries kholein.
  2. Recent Entries (#, Date, Source, Reference, Description, Amount) aur Account Summary (Total Debit, Total Credit, Net) parhein.
  3. Entry kholne ke liye us par dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aap ka post kiya hua har sale, purchase, payment aur expense yahan entry ki surat mein hota hai.

17.13 Manual Journal Entry post karna

Maqsad: Wo cheez darj karta hai jo kisi document mein nahi aati, maslan malik ka paisa lagana, bank charge ya accrual.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Journal Entries → New Entry

Tareeqa:

  1. New Entry dabayein.
  2. Date, chaahein to Reference aur Description likhein (misal: Depreciation for the month).
  3. Har line par Account chunein aur Debit ya Credit likhein; mazeed ke liye Add Line.
  4. Kul debit aur kul credit barabar karein.
  5. Post Entry dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Journal entry #N posted.' likhta hai aur wo list mein nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Entry is out of balance: debits X ≠ credits Y.Dono total barabar karein.
... is a control account — it is maintained by its subledger and cannot be posted to by hand.Receivables, Payables aur Inventory sirf documents se hilte hain.
<period> is closed. Post to an open period.Open mahina istemal karein.
A journal entry needs a description.Description likhein.
A journal entry needs at least two lines with amounts.Doosri line shamil karein.
Zaroori noteSirf wo post karein jo koi normal screen darj nahi kar sakti. Receivables, Payables aur Inventory control accounts hain: ye sirf documents se hilte hain, haath se kabhi nahi. Manual entry baad mein edit nahi hoti; sirf reverse ho sakti hai.

17.14 Journal Entry dekhna

Maqsad: Aik entry ki lines dikhata hai: code, account, debit aur credit.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Journal Entries → entry kholein

Tareeqa:

  1. List se entry kholein.
  2. Print ya Back dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Kul debit kul credit ke barabar hota hai.

17.15 Journal Entry reverse karna

Maqsad: Ulti entry post karta hai jo ghalat entry ko cancel kar de; dono record mein rehti hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Entry ke page par → Reverse this entry

Tareeqa:

  1. Entry kholein.
  2. Reverse this entry dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Entry #N reversed by #M. Both remain in the ledger.' likhta hai.

Zaroori noteEntry do baar reverse nahi hoti, aur reversal khud reverse nahi hoti. Documents (sales, purchases waghera) ki entries document se reverse hoti hain, yahan se nahi.

17.16 Fixed Asset Register

Maqsad: Assets ko acquisition date, method, cost, accumulated depreciation, net book value aur status ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fixed Assets

Tareeqa:

  1. Accounting → Fixed Assets kholein.
  2. Asset kholne ke liye us par dabayein; Print PDF register print karta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Net book value cost minus accumulated depreciation hoti hai.

17.17 Fixed Asset shamil karna

Maqsad: Istemal ke liye kharidi hui cheez, maslan gaari ya computer, darj karta hai aur purchase post karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fixed Assets → New Asset

Tareeqa:

  1. New Asset dabayein.
  2. Asset name (misal: Delivery Van), chaahein to Tag / Serial, Acquired on aur Cost likhein.
  3. Agar ho to Salvage value likhein, aur Paid from (cash ya bank account) chunein.
  4. Method chunein aur Useful life (months) (Straight Line) ya Rate (% per year) (Reducing Balance) bharein.
  5. Save & Post dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Fixed asset '<name>' recorded and posted.' likhta hai. Cash ya bank cost jitna kam hota hai; ye asset hai, kharch nahi.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
The asset needs a name.Naam likhein.
Cost must be greater than zero.Cost likhein.
Salvage value must be zero or more, and less than the cost.Salvage value durust karein.
Straight Line needs a useful life in months.Mahine likhein.
Reducing Balance needs a yearly rate.Rate likhein.
Choose the account that paid for the asset.Paid from chunein.

17.18 Mahana Depreciation chalana

Maqsad: Har munasib asset par aik mahine ki depreciation aik hi journal entry mein lagata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fixed Assets → Run Monthly Depreciation

Tareeqa:

  1. Month chunein.
  2. Post Depreciation dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Depreciation for <month year> posted — <amount>' likhta hai. Har asset ke page par charge nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Pick the month to depreciate.Mahina chunein.
Nothing to depreciate for <Month Year>.Wo mahina pehle post ho chuka ya koi asset munasib nahi.
Zaroori noteAik mahina sirf aik baar post hota hai. Mahine ke aakhir mein aik baar karein.

17.19 Fixed Asset dekhna

Maqsad: Aik asset ke mahana charges dikhata hai: mahina, charge, accumulated aur net book value.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Accounting → Fixed Assets → asset kholein

Tareeqa:

  1. Asset kholein.
  2. Table parhein; Print print karta hai; Back wapas jata hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har row apni journal entry se juri hoti hai.

17.20 Fixed Asset bechna ya khatam karna (Dispose)

Maqsad: Asset ko bechne ya kabaar karne ko darj karta hai aur book value ke muqable nafa ya nuqsan nikalta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Asset ke page par → Disposal date / Proceeds / Money received into → Dispose

Tareeqa:

  1. Asset kholein.
  2. Disposal date aur Proceeds likhein (kabaar ho to sifar).
  3. Money received into chunein (kuch na aaye to '— none (scrapped) —' rehne dein).
  4. Dispose dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message batata hai asset dispose ho gaya, nateeja (nafa ya nuqsan) aur journal entry number ke saath.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Check the disposal date and the proceeds.Numbers theek karein.
Proceeds cannot be negative.Sifar ya zyada likhein.
Choose the account the money was received into.Proceeds sifar se zyada hon to account chunein.

18. Reports

Har report menu Reports mein hai (manager aur admin). Statements general ledger se jama hote hain.

18.1 Sales aur Inventory Reports: khulasa

Maqsad: Date range ke liye Gross Sale, Sale Returns, Net Sales, Purchase Cost (COGS), Purchase Returns, Net Purchase Cost, Gross Profit aur Profit Margin dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Sales & Inventory kholein.
  2. Start Date aur End Date chunein.
  3. Generate Reports dabayein.
  4. Metric / Amount table parhein; Print istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Net Sales, Gross Sale minus Sale Returns ke barabar hota hai.

Zaroori noteGross Sale aur Net Sales alag figures hain: Net Sales sale returns ke baad hota hai. Number bataane se pehle label parhein.

18.2 Profit Reports (date, item, category, customer ke hisaab se)

Maqsad: Sale Amount aur Profit ko date, item, category aur customer ke hisaab se guroh bana kar dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory → Profit Reports tab (Generate Reports ke baad)

Tareeqa:

  1. Apni dates ke liye report generate karein.
  2. Profit Reports tab dabayein.
  3. Chaaron tables parhein; har ek mein Total row aur apne CSV aur Excel buttons hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har table ka Total Gross Sale figure ke barabar hota hai.

18.3 Purchase Reports (supplier, lines, payments)

Maqsad: Har supplier ki purchases (bills, kul miqdar, kul raqam), har purchase line, aur supplier payments dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory → Purchases tab

Tareeqa:

  1. Apni dates ke liye report generate karein.
  2. Purchases tab dabayein.
  3. Teeno tables parhein; unke CSV ya Excel buttons istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Purchases page se aik bill chunein aur use lines table mein dhoondein.

18.4 Sales Reports (lines aur receipts)

Maqsad: Range ki har sale line aur har customer receipt dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory → Sales tab

Tareeqa:

  1. Apni dates ke liye report generate karein.
  2. Sales tab dabayein.
  3. Tables parhein; CSV ya Excel istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Sales page se aik sale chunein aur use lines table mein dhoondein.

18.5 Returns Reports

Maqsad: Purchase returns aur sale returns ko miqdar, qeemat, total aur wajah ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory → Returns tab

Tareeqa:

  1. Apni dates ke liye report generate karein.
  2. Returns tab dabayein aur dono tables parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har return apne asal bill ya sale number ke saath nazar aata hai.

18.6 Parties Reports (supplier aur customer balances)

Maqsad: Har supplier (opening, bills, paid, balance, status) aur customer (opening, sales, received, balance, status) dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Sales & Inventory → Parties tab

Tareeqa:

  1. Parties tab dabayein.
  2. Supplier aur customer tables parhein; har ek par CSV aur Excel istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aik party chunein aur uska Balance uske ledger se milayein.

Zaroori noteCustomers page ka 'Sales' column sale returns ke baad ka hai; numbers milane se pehle har table ka column heading dekhein.

18.7 Profit & Loss Statement

Maqsad: Aik muddat ki income, operating expenses aur nateeja profit dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Profit & Loss

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Profit & Loss kholein.
  2. From aur To chunein aur Apply dabayein.
  3. Income aur Operating Expenses parhein; Print istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Range mein post kiya hua expense Operating Expenses ke neeche nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
The statement shows nothing.Range by-default 1 January se aaj tak hoti hai; use barhayein ya document dates aur reversal check karein.

18.8 Balance Sheet

Maqsad: Aik din par jo aap ke paas hai (Assets), jo dena hai (Liabilities) aur malikon ka hissa (Equity) dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Balance Sheet

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Balance Sheet kholein.
  2. Balance As Of chunein aur Show dabayein.
  3. Badge dekhein, Assets, Liabilities aur Equity parhein; Trial Balance wo report kholta hai.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Badge 'Balanced — Assets = Liabilities + Equity' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Out of balance by N.Reconciliation report kholein, backup download karein aur ittila dein; aisa nahi hona chahiye.

18.9 Trial Balance

Maqsad: Aik date par har account ka debit aur credit balance dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Trial Balance

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Trial Balance kholein.
  2. Balance As Of chunein, Show dabayein.
  3. Code, Account, Type, Debit, Credit parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Badge 'Balanced — Debits = Credits' likhta hai.

18.10 Cash Flow Statement

Maqsad: Dikhata hai cash asal mein kahan se aaya aur kahan gaya, taake nafa aur cash ka farq nazar aaye.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Cash Flow Statement

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Cash Flow Statement kholein.
  2. From aur To chunein, Apply dabayein.
  3. Sections aur Reconciliation box parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Badge 'Reconciled — opening + movement = closing' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Does not reconcile — a journal entry may be incomplete.Ruk jayein aur screenshot ke saath ittila dein.

18.11 AP/AR Aging

Maqsad: Dikhata hai kis ne aap ko aur aap ne kis ko dena hai, raqam ki umar ke hisaab se: 0–30, 31–60, 61–90 aur 90+ din.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → AP/AR Aging

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → AP/AR Aging kholein.
  2. Accounts Payable Aging (Suppliers) aur Accounts Receivable Aging (Customers) parhein; Print istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Aik party ka Total Due uske ledger balance se milayein.

18.12 Cash Book

Maqsad: Cash aur bank ki har harkat cash in aur cash out ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Cash Book

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Cash Book kholein.
  2. From, To aur chaahein to Method chunein, Apply dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Range ke liye Date, Type, Description, Method, Cash In aur Cash Out nazar aate hain.

18.13 Tax Summary

Maqsad: Muddat ke liye purchases par tax (Input Tax) aur sales par tax (Output Tax) dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Tax Summary

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Tax Summary kholein.
  2. From aur To chunein, Apply dabayein.
  3. Input Tax — Purchases aur Output Tax — Sales parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har document apni tax raqam ke saath nazar aata hai.

18.14 Subledger Reconciliation

Maqsad: General ledger ke har control account ka muqabla us ke neeche wale subledger se karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Reports → Reconciliation

Tareeqa:

  1. Reports → Reconciliation kholein.
  2. Control Account, Subledger, General Ledger, Difference aur Status parhein.
  3. Un customers aur suppliers ki list parhein jin se Accounts Receivable aur Accounts Payable bante hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har row ka Difference sifar aur Status achha hota hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
A row shows a Difference.Mazeed kuch post na karein; backup download karein aur screenshot ke saath ittila dein. Customer ya supplier ki manual ledger adjustment aik wajah ho sakti hai, kyunke wo general ledger mein post nahi hoti.

19. Kaam karne ke tareeqe

Rozmarra kaam, durustiyan aur masail ka hal. Ye boxes oopar ki sahulatein mila kar istemal karne ka tareeqa batate hain.

19.1 Pehli baar setup ki tarteeb

Maqsad: Nayi installation tayyar karne ki tarteeb.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Admin.

Tareeqa:

  1. Apne users banayein (Admin → Users) aur roles dein.
  2. Accounting → Chart of Accounts aur Fiscal Years & Periods dekhein.
  3. Apne cash aur bank accounts (Admin → Financial Accounts) opening balances ke saath shamil karein.
  4. Apni business categories enable karein (Admin → Business Configuration); agar dawaiyan ya expiry wala maal bechte hain to batch tracking aur expiry on karein.
  5. Agar aik se zyada warehouse hon to shamil karein (Admin → Warehouses) aur zaroorat ho to users ko mehdood karein (Admin → Location Access).
  6. Items (ya Bulk Import) opening stock ke saath shamil karein, phir suppliers aur customers opening balances ke saath.
  7. Balance Sheet kholein; Balanced likha hona chahiye.
  8. Uske baad hi purchases, sales aur payments darj karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Balance Sheet Balanced hai aur Stock Valuation aap ka opening stock dikhata hai.

Zaroori noteOpening balances asli accounting entries hain; karobar se pehle darj karein.

19.2 Mahine ke aakhir ki tarteeb

Maqsad: Mahina itminan se band karne ki checklist.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager ya admin; period close karna sirf admin.

Tareeqa:

  1. Mahine ka har invoice, payment, receipt aur expense darj karein, aur har draft post karein.
  2. Mahine ke liye Accounting → Fixed Assets → Run Monthly Depreciation chalayein.
  3. Reports → Reconciliation kholein; har Difference sifar hona chahiye. Warna ruk kar wajah dhoondein.
  4. Reports → Balance Sheet kholein; Balanced likha hona chahiye; Profit & Loss parhein.
  5. Backup download karein (Admin → Backup & Restore).
  6. Chaahein to period close karein (Fiscal Years & Periods → Close) taake uske figures na badlein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Reconciliation aur Balance Sheet sabz hain aur taaza backup file maujood hai.

19.3 Saal ke aakhir ki tarteeb

Maqsad: Saal ke aakhri mahine ke baad kya karna hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Admin.

Tareeqa:

  1. Aakhri mahine ke liye month-end tarteeb mukammal karein.
  2. Fiscal Years & Periods kholein aur Close Year dabayein.
  3. Agle fiscal year ko, agar na ho, Create fiscal year se banayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Balance Sheet badle baghair aagay chalti hai aur naye saal ka Profit & Loss sifar se shuru hota hai.

Zaroori noteNaye saal ke liye invoice numbers dobara shuru hote hain.

19.4 Ghalti theek karna: edit, correct, reverse ya return?

Maqsad: Ghalat document ko sahi karne ka durust tareeqa chunne mein madad karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab parhte hain; edit manager ke liye, correct aur reverse admin ke liye.

Tareeqa:

  1. Draft purchase ya sale: edit karein (manager) ya delete karein (admin). Kuch book nahi hua tha.
  2. Chhoti ghalti wali post shuda purchase ya sale: admin wajah ke saath Correct istemal karta hai.
  3. Post shuda document jo hona hi nahi chahiye tha, ya koi payment, receipt, expense, return ya stock adjustment: admin Reverse dabata hai, phir aap sahi tarah dobara darj karein.
  4. Maal asal mein supplier ko wapas gaya ya customer se wapas aaya: Purchase Return ya Sale Return darj karein, reversal nahi.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Ghalat document reversed ya corrected dikhta hai aur sahi wala list mein hota hai.

Zaroori noteReversal radd ho sakta hai jab usay wapas karne se stock manfi ho jaye, maslan us purchase ka reversal jis ka maal bik chuka ho.

19.5 Masail: koi figure ghalat lagta hai

Maqsad: Jab report aap ki umeed se alag ho to pehle kahan dekhna hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab.

Tareeqa:

  1. Report ki date range dekhein; us se bahar ki date wala document shamil nahi hota.
  2. Document ka Status dekhein: draft gina nahi jata; reversed shamil nahi hota.
  3. Stock pages par warehouse filter dekhein.
  4. Stock ki qeemat ke liye yaad rakhein ke cost weighted average hai, aakhri ada ki hui qeemat nahi.
  5. Reports → Reconciliation kholein; agar Difference nazar aaye to post karna band karein aur ittila dein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Filter theek karne ya draft post karne ke baad figure mil jata hai.

19.6 Masail: system koi kaam karne se inkaar karta hai

Maqsad: Aam inkaar ke messages ka matlab.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sab.

Tareeqa:

  1. Message parhein; wo wajah batata hai.
  2. '... is posted to the general ledger and can no longer be changed. Reverse it instead.' — Reverse (admin) ya Correct (admin) istemal karein.
  3. 'Insufficient stock' — chune hue warehouse mein document ke liye kam maal hai.
  4. '... is closed. Post to an open period.' — open mahine ki date istemal karein ya admin se reopen karwayein.
  5. 'Access denied' ya dhundla menu item — aap ka role ye nahi kar sakta; manager ya admin se kahein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Durusti ke baad wahi kaam qabool ho jata hai.

19.7 Mehfooz tareeqe: backups aur ehtiyat

Maqsad: Aisi aadatein jo aap ke data ko mehfooz rakhti hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Admin.

Tareeqa:

  1. Har hafte backup download karein (Admin → Backup & Restore), aur restore, reset ya year close se pehle.
  2. Backups doosre computer ya drive par rakhein.
  3. Developer Tasks ka password ya admin account kisi se share na karein.
  4. Live system par Delete Transactions ya Delete Everything tab tak istemal na karein jab tak naye sire se shuru karna na ho.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Mukhya computer ke bahar tareekh wali backup file maujood hai.

20. HR: employees, departments aur designations

Menu HR & Payroll (optional module). Ye menus tab hi nazar aate hain jab technical staff ne module on kiya ho (Developer Tasks → Optional Modules). HR ke tamam modules by-default off hote hain.

20.1 Employees dekhna aur filter karna

Maqsad: Employees ko code, naam, department, designation, joining date, status, basic salary aur active flag ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employees

Tareeqa:

  1. HR & Payroll → Employees kholein.
  2. Search (naam, code, phone ya email) mein likhein aur/ya Department, Designation, Status ya Active chunein, phir Filter dabayein.
  3. Print PDF, ya New Employee, Departments aur Designations buttons istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Employees code ki tarteeb mein nazar aate hain.

20.2 Employee shamil karna

Maqsad: Employee ka record banata hai. Employee code system khud deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employees → New Employee

Tareeqa:

  1. New Employee dabayein.
  2. Name aur Joining Date (zaroori) likhein; chaahein to CNIC, Department, Designation, Phone, Email, Address.
  3. Employment Status chunein: Permanent, Probation, Contract ya Intern.
  4. Chaahein to Basic Salary, Allowances aur Deductions likhein; Active employee par tick rehne dein.
  5. Chaahein to Documents held aur Notes likhein.
  6. Save button dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Employee <code> added.' likhta hai.

Zaroori notePayroll ye salary boxes nahi parhta: wo employee ka Salary Structure istemal karta hai (Payroll dekhein).

20.3 Employee ka Profile dekhna

Maqsad: Aik employee ki tafseel aur salary figures dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employees → employee par click

Tareeqa:

  1. Employees kholein aur employee par click karein.

  3. Badalne ke liye Edit, ya Back dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Tafseel employee form se milti hai.

20.4 Employee edit karna

Maqsad: Employee ki tafseel badalta hai. Employee code kabhi nahi badalta.

Kahan milega: Employee ke profile par → Edit

Tareeqa:

  1. Profile par Edit dabayein.
  2. Fields badlein.
  3. Save button dabayein (ya Cancel).

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Employee <code> updated.' likhta hai.

20.5 Employee delete karna

Maqsad: Aise employee ko hata deta hai jis ki koi tareekh nahi.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employees → delete button

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Employee <code> deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... has ... history, so the record was deactivated instead of deleted.Jis employee ki attendance, leave ya payroll ki tareekh ho use hataya nahi balkay inactive kiya jata hai.

20.6 Employee ko Login se jorna

... is already linked to ...Aik login sirf aik employee ka ho sakta hai.
That user was not found.List mein se chunein.

20.7 Departments: dekhna aur shamil karna

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Department name is required.Naam likhein.
Department '...' already exists.Maujooda istemal karein.

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
'...' still has employees, so it cannot be deleted.Pehle employees ko doosre department mein muntaqil karein.

20.9 Designations: dekhna aur shamil karna

22. Leave (Chhutti)

Menu HR & Payroll (optional Leave module; HR zaroori). Ye menus tab hi nazar aate hain jab technical staff ne module on kiya ho (Developer Tasks → Optional Modules). HR ke tamam modules by-default off hote hain.

22.1 Leave Balances

Maqsad: Har employee ke liye har leave type mein allocated, used aur remaining din, aur pending, approved aur rejected ginti dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user.

Tareeqa:

  1. Leave Balances kholein.
  2. Employee aur Year chunein, Show dabayein.
  3. Print PDF istemal karein; buttons New Request, Requests, Allocations aur Types par le jate hain.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Remaining, Allocated minus Used ke barabar hota hai.

22.2 Leave Types: dekhna aur shamil karna

Maqsad: Chhutti ki qismein define karta hai, maslan casual ya medical, aur ye ke wo paid hain ya allocation se nikalti hain.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Koi bhi verified user dekhta hai; admin shamil karta hai.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Leave Types

Tareeqa:

  1. Leave Types kholein.
  2. Code (misal: HAJJ) aur Name likhein.
  3. Zaroorat ke mutabiq Paid leave, Drawn from an allocation, Carry forward allowed par tick karein; chaahein to Max days per year, Carry-forward limit aur Description bharein.
  4. Add Type dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Leave type '<name>' added.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
You do not have permission to configure leave types.Sirf admin kar sakta hai.

22.3 Leave Type active ya inactive karna

Maqsad: Type ko delete kiye baghair pesh hone se rokta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Leave Types → row ka toggle button

Tareeqa:

  1. Type ka toggle button dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message ''<name>' is now active.' ya 'inactive.' likhta hai.

22.4 Leave Type delete karna

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Tareeqa:

  1. Row ka delete button dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Leave type '<name>' deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
'...' is a standard type and cannot be deleted. Deactivate it instead.Toggle istemal karein.
'...' has leave history, so it was deactivated rather than deleted.Kuch karne ki zaroorat nahi.

22.5 Employee ko Leave ke din allocate karna

Only a pending request can be approved.Darkhwast par faisla ho chuka hai.

23. Payroll (Tankhwah)

Menu HR & Payroll (optional Payroll module; HR zaroori). Manager aur admin payroll dekhte aur tayyar karte hain; sirf admin finalize, pay, cancel ya payments reverse karta hai, kyunke ye general ledger mein likhte hain. Ye menus tab hi nazar aate hain jab technical staff ne module on kiya ho (Developer Tasks → Optional Modules). HR ke tamam modules by-default off hote hain.

23.1 Salary Structures dekhna

Maqsad: Har employee ka salary structure dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Salary Structures

Tareeqa:

  1. Salary Structures kholein.
  2. Code, Employee, Effective From, Basic aur Components parhein.
  3. Row par Edit dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Jis employee ko tankhwah deni hai uska structure nazar aata hai.

23.2 Employee ka Salary Structure set karna

Maqsad: Basic Salary, allowances, bonus aur fixed deductions ki raqmein set karta hai jo payroll istemal karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Salary Structures → Edit

Tareeqa:

  1. Employee par Edit dabayein.
  2. Har component ke saamne raqam likhein (Basic Salary, House Rent Allowance, Medical Allowance, Conveyance Allowance, Other Allowance, Bonus, Tax, Loan Deduction, Other Deduction).
  3. Chaahein to Notes likhein.
  4. Save Structure dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Salary structure saved for <employee>.' likhta hai.

Zaroori noteOvertime, advance recovery aur absence deductions attendance, leave aur advances se khud nikalte hain.

23.3 Payroll Period banana

Maqsad: Tankhwah ka period, maslan aik mahina, kholta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Payroll → New Period

Tareeqa:

  1. Payroll kholein aur New Period dabayein.
  2. Period Name (misal: January 2026), Start Date aur End Date likhein; chaahein to Notes.
  3. Create Period dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Payroll period '<name>' created.' likhta hai aur wo Payroll page par Draft ki surat mein nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Those exact dates already exist as a payroll period.Maujooda period istemal karein.

23.4 Period ki Payroll process karna

Maqsad: Har us employee ki payslip hisaab karta hai jis ka salary structure ho. Jab tak aap mutmain na hon dohraya ja sakta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Payroll → period Open karein → Process Payroll (Recalculate)

Tareeqa:

  1. Period kholein.
  2. Process Payroll dabayein (process ho chuka ho to Recalculate likha hota hai).
  3. Har employee ke Days, OT (h), Gross, Deductions aur Net parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message '<period> processed — N payslip(s) calculated' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Skipped <employee>: ... no active salary structureUs employee ko Salary Structure dein aur dobara process karein.
Zaroori noteAttendance tab hi istemal hoti hai jab Attendance module on ho; off ho to har employee ko poora period milta hai.

23.5 Aik Employee ki Payroll ka Preview

Maqsad: Dikhata hai aik employee ki tankhwah aik period ke liye kaise hisaab hogi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Payroll period ke page par → employee ki row

Tareeqa:

  1. Period kholein aur employee ka preview link istemal karein.
  2. Hisaab parhein; Back dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Preview process ke baad ki payslip se milta hai.

23.6 Payroll Period finalize karna

Maqsad: Payslips ko lock karta hai aur period ki aik journal entry post karta hai: salary expense debit, salaries payable, tax payable aur advances credit.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Payroll period ke page par → Finalize

Tareeqa:

  1. Yaqeeni banayein period process aur check ho chuka hai.
  2. Finalize dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai period finalize ho gaya; View Journal Entry nazar aata hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Nothing to finalize — process the period first.Pehle Process Payroll dabayein.
... was not finalized — accounting refused the entry: ...Wajah parhein (maslan band period).
Zaroori notePeriod finalize hone par admins ko notification milti hai.

23.7 Tankhwah ada karna

Maqsad: Cash ya bank account se asal mein di gayi tankhwah darj karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Finalize shuda payroll period ke page par → Pay Salary

Tareeqa:

  1. Finalize shuda period kholein.
  2. Amount, Payment Date aur chaahein to Reference likhein, aur account chunein.
  3. Pay Salary dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai period ke liye salary payment darj ho gayi; payment period ke payments table mein nazar aati hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
Pick the cash or bank account the salary was paid from.Account chunein.
Payment amount must be more than zero.Raqam likhein.
<period> is already fully paid.Dene ko kuch baqi nahi.
only a finalized ...Pehle period finalize karein.

23.8 Salary Payment reverse karna

Maqsad: Darj shuda salary payment cancel karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Period ke payments table mein → reverse button

Tareeqa:

  1. Payment par reverse button dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Salary payment reversed' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
That payment has already been reversed.Kuch karne ki zaroorat nahi.

23.9 Payroll Period cancel karna

Maqsad: Finalize shuda period ko uski journal entry reverse kar ke wapas karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Period ke page par → Cancel Period

Tareeqa:

  1. Pehle koi bhi salary payments reverse karein.
  2. Cancel Period dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message kehta hai period cancel ho gaya; admins ko notification milti hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... cannot be cancelled because salary payments exist.Pehle payments reverse karein.

23.10 Payroll Period delete karna

Maqsad: Aise period ko hata deta hai jo kabhi finalize nahi hua.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Sirf admin.

Kahan milega: Period ke page par → Delete

Tareeqa:

  1. Delete dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Payroll period '<name>' deleted.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
... is finalized and cannot be deleted. Cancel it instead.Cancel Period istemal karein.

23.11 Payslip dekhna aur print karna

Maqsad: Aik employee ki aik period ki payslip dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: Period ke page par → employee row ka Payslip link

Tareeqa:

  1. Period kholein aur payslip link dabayein.
  2. Print, ya Back dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Dikhayi gayi Net Salary period page ke Net ke barabar hoti hai.

23.12 Employee Advance darj karna

Maqsad: Employee ko di gayi raqam darj karta hai jo payroll qiston mein wasool karegi.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employee Advances

Tareeqa:

  1. Employee Advances kholein.
  2. Employee, Date, Amount, chaahein to Instalment per period aur Remarks chunein.
  3. Record dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Advance recorded for <employee>.' likhta hai aur row Amount, Recovered aur Outstanding dikhati hai.

23.13 Employee Advance cancel karna

Maqsad: Ghalti se darj hui advance cancel karta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Manager aur admin.

Kahan milega: HR & Payroll → Employee Advances → cancel button

Tareeqa:

  1. Row ka cancel button dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Advance cancelled.' likhta hai.

24. My Work (employee ki apni sahulat)

My Work menu sirf us user ko nazar aata hai jis ka login kisi employee record se juda ho, aur sirf un modules ke liye jo on hon.

24.1 My Work: Mera Profile

Maqsad: Aap ki apni employee tafseel dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Profile

Tareeqa:

  1. My Work → My Profile kholein.
  2. My Attendance, My Leaves, My Payslips buttons istemal karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Department, Designation, Joining Date, Status aur contact details nazar aati hain.

24.2 My Work: Meri Attendance

Maqsad: Aik mahine ki aap ki attendance dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Attendance

Tareeqa:

  1. My Attendance kholein.
  2. Month aur Year chunein, Show dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Date, Status, In, Out, Hours, Overtime aur Remarks nazar aate hain.

24.3 My Work: Meri Chhuttiyan

Maqsad: Aap ke leave balances aur darkhwastein dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Leaves

Tareeqa:

  1. My Leaves kholein.
  2. Saal chunein.
  3. Allocated, Used, Remaining aur apni darkhwastein status ke saath parhein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Remaining, Allocated minus Used ke barabar hota hai.

24.4 My Work: Chhutti ki Darkhwast

Maqsad: Aap ko apne liye chhutti maangne deta hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Leaves → Request Leave

Tareeqa:

  1. Request Leave dabayein.
  2. Leave Type, Duration, Start Date, End Date aur chaahein to Reason chunein.
  3. Submit Request dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Leave request submitted for approval (N day(s)).' likhta hai.

Zaroori noteFaisla manager ya admin karta hai; aap ko faisle ki notification milti hai.

24.5 My Work: Chhutti ki Darkhwast wapas lena

Maqsad: Aap ki di hui darkhwast wapas leta hai, jab tak wapas ho sakti ho.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Leaves → darkhwast par withdraw button

Tareeqa:

  1. Darkhwast par withdraw button dabayein aur tasdeeq karein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Message 'Leave request withdrawn.' likhta hai.

Aam ghaltiyan:

Aap kya dekhte hainKya karein
This request is <status> and can no longer be ...Sirf shuruaati marhale ki darkhwast wapas hoti hai; manager se kahein.

24.6 My Work: Meri Payslips

Maqsad: Aap ki payslips period ke hisaab se gross, deductions aur net ke saath dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Payslips

Tareeqa:

  1. My Payslips kholein.
  2. Period par View dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Har row Period, From, To, Gross, Deductions aur Net dikhati hai.

24.7 My Work: Meri Payslip dekhna aur print karna

Maqsad: Aap ki aik payslip print ke liye dikhata hai.

Kaun istemal kar sakta hai: Employee se juda user.

Kahan milega: My Work → My Payslips → View

Tareeqa:

  1. Payslip par View dabayein.
  2. Print, ya Back dabayein.

Tasdeeq kaise karein: Net Salary oopar nazar aati hai.
